Conclusion: EMiX为大规模多核架构提供了一个可扩展、开源的多FPGA仿真平台，有效解决了单FPGA资源瓶颈问题，推动了芯片设计验证技术的发展。

Abstract: FPGA-level emulation is a key step in pre-silicon chip design validation. However, emulating large-scale multi-core systems increasingly exceed the hardware resource capacity of a single FPGA, limiting the feasibility of full-system emulation. To address this challenge, we introduce EMiX, a scalable multi-FPGA framework that enables distributed emulation of multi-core RISC-V architectures beyond single-FPGA resource limits. EMiX systematically partitions a monolithic multi-core design into multiple components and deploys them across multiple interconnected FPGAs, effectively exploiting inter-FPGA interconnects to balance scalability and performance without requiring fundamental RTL redesign. We prototype EMiX with a 64-core architecture across eight interconnected Alveo U55c FPGAs (scalable on core and FPGA counts), successfully demonstrating full-system execution including Linux boot. EMiX will be released as an open-source platform.

</details>


### [93] [Verification and Validation (V&V)-in-the-Loop for RISC-V Design: The Holistic Vision of BZL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27013)
*Sajjad Ahmed,Alexander Kropotov,Roberto Ignacio Genovese,Bernat Homs,Eloi Merino,Francesco Urbani,Henrique Yano,Iván Díaz,Joan Gracia Fernandez,Matteo Toselli,Muhammad Imran,Muhammad Abu Bakar Umar Haider Iqbal,Nadeem Yaseen,Quswar Abid,Shaista Cheema,Samuel Sanchez,Daniel Garcia,Joan Cabré,Mostafa Elyasi,Fernando Ayats,Miquel Moreto,Teresa Cervero,Oscar Palomar,Behzad Salami*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴塞罗那Zetascale实验室（BZL）项目针对RISC-V高性能计算芯片设计提出的一种整体性硅前验证与确认（V&V）方法。该方法整合了UVM验证环境、FPGA验证平台和CI/CD自动化流程，旨在确保芯片设计的功能正确性和系统级可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加强欧洲在基于RISC-V的高性能计算芯片设计与制造方面的能力，需要一个能够确保高度稳健的RISC-V芯片设计的综合验证与确认方法论，以保证硅前功能正确性和系统级验证的有效性。

Method: 提出了一种集成的硅前验证与确认流程，包含三个互补平台：(1) 基于UVM的验证环境，用于彻底验证RTL功能；(2) 基于FPGA的验证平台，用于系统级软硬件RTL验证；(3) CI/CD流程，用于跨领域持续自动化构建、部署和测试。该方法利用大规模CPU和FPGA硬件基础设施嵌入工业级V&V循环中。

Result: 通过将上述平台整合到工业级验证循环中，BZL项目实现了硬件开发和软件集成的持续演化与可靠性保障。该V&V流程为RISC-V芯片设计的硅前功能正确性和系统级验证提供了稳健且可扩展的基础。

Conclusion: BZL的V&V流程可作为欧洲战略计划（如EPI和DARE）及其他类似项目的关键推动力，为RISC-V芯片设计的硅前验证提供了一个稳健、可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: The Barcelona Zetascale Lab (BZL) project aims to strengthening Europe's capacity in the design and manufacture of RISC-V based high-performance computing chips. In this context, we present a holistic pre-silicon verification and validation (V&V) methodology targeting highly robust RISC-V chip designs. This paper provides an overview of BZL's V&V approach, which integrates three complementary platforms: (1) a UVM-based verification environment to thoroughly validate RTL functionality; (2) an FPGA-based validation platform that enables system-level pre-silicon hardware-software RTL validation; and (3) a CI/CD flow that continuously automates build, deployment, and tests across these domains. By embedding these platforms into an industrial-grade V&V loop and exploiting large-scale CPU and FPGA hardware infrastructures, the BZL project enables continuous evolution of reliable hardware development and software integration. We believe that the BZL's V&V flow represents a robust and scalable foundation for ensuring the pre-silicon functional correctness and system level validation of RISC-V chip designs, and can serve as a key enabler for strategic initiatives in Europe, such as EPI and DARE, and beyond.

</details>


### [94] [RCW-CIM: A Digital CIM-based LLM Accelerator with Read-Compute/Write](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27384)
*Yan-Cheng Guo,Tian-Sheuan Chang,Jian-Wei Su*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RCW-CIM架构，通过读-计算/写架构、非线性算子融合和WS-OCS数据流，有效降低了大语言模型推理中的权重更新延迟和内存访问开销，实现了高能效和高吞吐的硬件加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字计算存储(DCIM)架构在加速大语言模型时往往忽视权重更新延迟问题。由于LLM权重远超单个CIM宏容量，频繁的权重更新成为性能瓶颈，亟需优化架构以降低延迟并提升能效。

Method: 提出读-计算/写(RCW)架构以最小化权重更新延迟；引入非线性算子融合机制，通过部分累加和分组近似技术缓解依赖引起的延迟；设计权重驻留-输出列驻留(WS-OCS)数据流，减少外部DRAM访问和内部CIM权重更新。

Result: RCW架构在Llama2-7B模型上降低解码计算延迟21.59%；非线性算子融合实现延迟降低69.17%；WS-OCS数据流在1024 tokens预填充阶段分别减少51.6%外部DRAM访问和87.6%内部CIM权重更新，整体延迟降低49.76%。基于TSMC 22nm工艺，100MHz工作频率下实现3.28 TOPS算力和42.3 TOPS/W能效，对INT4权重Llama2模型实现4.2ms预填充延迟和26.87 tokens/s解码速度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的RCW-CIM架构成功解决了LLM加速中的权重更新延迟瓶颈，通过架构创新和数据流优化显著提升了性能和能效，为大语言模型的高效硬件加速提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Digital computing-in-memory (DCIM) has emerged as a promising solution for large language model (LLM) acceleration by minimizing data transfers between external DRAM and on-chip accelerators while maintaining high precision for superior accuracy. However, existing CIM architectures often overlook weight update latency, which becomes critical as LLM weights are far larger than a single CIM macro capacity. To address this issue, this paper proposes a read-compute/write (RCW) architecture that effectively minimizes weight update latency, along with a nonlinear operator fusion that further mitigates dependencyinduced latency. The proposed RCW reduces decoding computing latency by 21.59% on the Llama2-7B model. In addition, the nonlinear operator fusion mechanism achieves a 69.17% latency reduction through efficient partial accumulation and group-based approximation. Furthermore, a weight-stationary and output column stationary (WS-OCS) dataflow is introduced to reduce both external DRAM access and internal CIM weight updates by 51.6% and 87.6% respectively during the prefill phase of 1024 tokens, leading to an overall 49.76% latency reduction. Fabricated using TSMC 22 nm CMOS technology and operating at 100 MHz, the proposed RCW-CIM achieves 3.28 TOPS and 42.3 TOPS/W, enabling 4.2 ms prefill latency and 26.87 decoded tokens per second for the INT4-weight Llama2 model with dual DDR5-6400 memory.

</details>


### [95] [VitaLLM: A Versatile, Ultra-Compact Ternary LLM Accelerator with Dependency-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27396)
*Zi-Wei Lin,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出VitaLLM加速器，通过软硬件协同设计解决三元量化LLM在边缘设备上的部署瓶颈，在16nm工艺下实现17.4 TOPS/mm²/W的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署大语言模型面临内存带宽和功耗的关键瓶颈。虽然三元量化（如BitNet b1.58）能显著减小模型尺寸，但其在通用硬件上的直接部署仍受限于负载不均衡、带宽受限的解码过程以及严格的数据依赖性。

Method: 提出VitaLLM软硬件协同设计加速器：1）异构双核计算策略，结合专用TINT-Core处理大规模三元投影和统一BoothFlex-Core处理混合精度注意力；2）前导一预测（LOP）机制修剪冗余KV缓存获取；3）依赖感知调度框架隐藏非线性操作延迟；4）扩展位串行设计（BoothFlex-BS）实现精度敏捷推理。

Result: 在TSMC 16nm工艺下实现，解码吞吐量达70.70 tokens/s，面积仅0.223 mm²，功耗65.97 mW，性能指标（FOM）达17.4 TOPS/mm²/W，显著超越现有最先进加速器。

Conclusion: VitaLLM成功解决了三元LLM在边缘设备部署中的核心挑战，通过异构计算架构和优化机制实现了高能效推理，为资源受限环境下的大语言模型部署提供了有效的解决方案，并展现了架构对精度敏捷推理的适应性。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices faces critical bottlenecks in memory bandwidth and power consumption. While ternary quantization (e.g., BitNet b1.58) significantly reduces model size, its direct deployment on general-purpose hardware is hindered by workload imbalance, bandwidth-bound decoding, and strict data dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{VitaLLM}, a hardware-software co-designed accelerator tailored for efficient ternary LLM inference. We introduce a heterogeneous \textbf{Dual-Core Compute Strategy} that synergizes specialized TINT-Cores for massive ternary projections with a unified BoothFlex-Core for mixed-precision attention, ensuring high utilization across both compute-bound prefill and bandwidth-bound decode stages. Furthermore, we develop a \textbf{Leading One Prediction (LOP)} mechanism to prune redundant Key-Value (KV) cache fetches and a \textbf{Dependency-Aware Scheduling} framework to hide the latency of nonlinear operations. Implemented in TSMC 16nm technology, VitaLLM achieves a decoding throughput of 70.70 tokens/s within an ultra-compact area of 0.223 mm$^2$ and a power consumption of 65.97 mW. The design delivers a superior Figure of Merit (FOM) of 17.4 TOPS/mm$^2$/W, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art accelerators. Finally, we explore an extended bit-serial design (BoothFlex-BS) to demonstrate the architecture's adaptability for precision-agile inference.

</details>


### [96] [CuLifter: Lifting GPU Binaries to Typed IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27486)
*Jisheng Zhao,Huanzhi Pu,Shinnung Jeong,Chihyo Ahn,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CuLifter，一个SASS到LLVM IR的提升框架，通过约束传播与冲突检测恢复寄存器类型，解决了GPU二进制提升中因统一寄存器文件导致的类型信息丢失这一核心挑战，在24,437个GPU函数上实现了99.98%的成功提升率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU编译器将所有数据类型合并到统一的寄存器文件中，导致二进制分析工具依赖的类型信息丢失，这使得GPU二进制提升面临核心挑战：如何从无类型的寄存器文件中恢复类型信息。

Method: 提出CuLifter框架，包含三个关键技术：通过约束传播与冲突检测恢复寄存器类型、重构显式控制流、聚合多指令模式。其中类型恢复是核心步骤，通过约束传播在指令间推断寄存器类型并在冲突时进行检测。

Result: 在8个基准测试套件（涵盖开源应用、厂商库和优化ML运行时的919个cubin中共24,437个GPU函数）上，CuLifter成功将99.98%的函数提升为有效的LLVM IR。消融研究表明，禁用类型恢复会使x86通过率从73.8%降至0%，下降73.8个百分点，证实类型恢复是产生语义正确IR的唯一必需步骤。

Conclusion: 类型恢复是GPU二进制提升的核心挑战和关键解决方案。CuLifter通过约束传播与冲突检测有效解决了统一寄存器文件带来的类型信息丢失问题，能够大规模可靠地将GPU二进制代码提升为语义正确的LLVM IR，为GPU二进制分析工具提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: GPU compilers merge all data types into a single unified register file, erasing the type information that binary-analysis tools rely on. We show that type recovery from this untyped register file is the central challenge of GPU binary lifting. We present CuLifter, a SASS-to-LLVM IR lifting framework that recovers register types via constraint propagation with conflict detection, reconstructs explicit control flow, and aggregates multi-instruction patterns. Across eight benchmark suites (24,437 GPU functions in 919 cubins) spanning open-source applications, vendor libraries, and optimized ML runtimes, CuLifter successfully lifts 99.98% of functions to valid LLVM IR. An ablation study confirms that type recovery is the only step required to produce semantically correct IR: disabling it drops the x86 pass rate from 73.8% to 0%, a 73.8 percentage-point drop.

</details>


### [97] [HAVEN: Hybrid Automated Verification ENgine for UVM Testbench Synthesis with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27643)
*Chang-Chih Meng,Yu-Ren Lu,Guan-Yu Lin,Tsung Tai Yeh,Kai-Chiang Wu,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HAVEN系统，通过模板引擎和协议感知DSL避免LLM直接编写HDL代码，实现了SOTA的UVM测试平台生成性能（100%编译成功率、90.6%代码覆盖率、87.9%功能覆盖率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC验证占据开发周期近70%，现有基于LLM的测试平台生成方法由于HDL训练数据稀缺而难以生成正确代码，亟需一种能够避免LLM直接编写HDL的解决方案。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）利用LLM代理分析设计规范生成架构计划，结合预定义的协议特定Jinja2模板引擎生成所有UVM组件；2）引入协议感知序列DSL将序列分解为细粒度步骤类型，先用预定义DSL模式建立高覆盖率序列，再迭代利用LLM分析覆盖率缺口并生成针对性DSL序列。

Result: 在19个开源IP设计（涵盖Direct、Wishbone、AXI4-Lite三种接口协议）上实验表明：HAVEN实现100%编译成功率、平均90.6%代码覆盖率、87.9%功能覆盖率，在LLM辅助测试平台生成系统中达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: HAVEN是首个利用预定义协议特定模板生成所有UVM组件并使用协议感知DSL和基于规则的代码生成器生成UVM序列的系统，成功解决了LLM直接生成HDL代码的准确性问题，为IC验证自动化提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Integrated Circuit (IC) verification consumes nearly 70% of the IC development cycle, and recent research leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate testbenches and reduce verification overhead. However, LLMs have difficulty generating testbenches correctly. Unlike high-level programming languages, Hardware Description Languages (HDLs) are extremely rare in LLMs training data, leading LLMs to produce incorrect code. To overcome challenges when using LLMs to generate Universal Verification Methodology (UVM) testbenches and sequences, wepropose HAVEN (Hybrid Automated Verification ENgine) to prevent LLMs from writing HDL directly. For UVM testbench generation, HAVEN utilizes LLM agents to analyze design specifications to produce a structured architectural plan. The HAVEN Template Engine then combines with predefined and protocol-specific templates to generate all UVM components with correct bus-handshake timing. For UVM sequence generation, HAVEN introduces a Protocol-Aware Sequence Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that decomposes sequences into fine-grained step types. A set of predefined DSL patterns first establishes sequences that achieve a high coverage rate without LLM involvement. HAVEN continues to improve the coverage rate by iteratively leveraging LLM agents to analyze coverage gap reports and compose additional targeted DSL sequences. Unlike previous works, HAVEN is the first system that utilizes pre-defined, protocol-specific Jinja2 templates to generate all UVM components and UVM sequences using our proposed Protocol-Aware DSL and rule-based code generator. Our experimental results on 19 open-source IP designs spanning three interface protocols (Direct, Wishbone, AXI4-Lite) show that HAVEN achieves 100% compilation success, 90.6% code coverage, and 87.9% functional coverage on average, and is SOTA among LLM-assisted testbench generation systems.

</details>


### [98] [RuC: HDL-Agnostic Rule Completion Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27780)
*Arnau Ayguadé Domingo,Miquel Alberti-Binimelis,Cristian Gutierrez-Gomez,Emanuele Parisi,Razine Moundir Ghorab,Miquel Moreto,Gokcen Kestor,Dario Garcia-Gasulla*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了RuC框架，一种语法驱动的规则选择基准生成器，用于生成细粒度、可控的RTL代码补全任务，并发现FIM提示策略效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM在RTL代码补全基准测试中，要么评估整个硬件模块的生成，要么评估模块内单行的补全，缺乏对代码补全样本粒度和语法补全范围的控制能力。

Method: 提出了语言无关的规则补全框架，利用目标HDL语法掩码语法定义的代码区域，并提示模型使用周围未掩码的代码作为上下文重新生成它们。基于Tiny Tapeout TT07和CVE2 RISC-V核心生成了两个SystemVerilog规则补全基准。

Result: 代码补全性能强烈依赖于模型类型、掩码区域的语法结构和提示策略。具体而言，Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) 提示获得了最高分数。

Conclusion: 语法驱动的、任意粒度的基准测试对于有意义的评估LLM在RTL开发工作流中的能力具有重要价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly improved in performance across code-related tasks, making their integration into Register Transfer Level (RTL) development increasingly attractive. Mimicking the behavior of inline code assistants, many benchmarks evaluate LLMs' capabilities in code completion, either assessing the generation of entire hardware modules or the completion of a single line within a module. However both of these approaches lack the ability to control the granularity of the code-completion sample size and the syntactic range of completions. To overcome these limitations, we present a framework for language-agnostic rule completion (RuC), a grammar-driven, rule-selectable benchmark generator that automatically produces RTL code-completion tasks from a set of input hardware description sources. RuC uses the target Hardware Description Language (HDL) grammar to mask syntactically defined code regions and prompts a model to regenerate them using the surrounding unmasked code as context, enabling a controlled and scalable evaluation of the domain-specific model's code-understanding capabilities, ranging from assignments to the reconstruction of entire logic blocks. We use RuC to generate two SystemVerilog rule-completion benchmarks from the Tiny Tapeout shuttle TT07 and the CVE2 RISC-V core to demonstrate RuC's applicability to a broad range of designs, and conduct a comparative study of the code completion capabilities of modern open-source LLMs across diverse settings. Results indicate that completion performance strongly depends on the model type, the grammatical structure of the masked region, and the prompting strategy. Specifically, the highest scores are obtained with Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) prompting. These findings highlight the value of grammar-driven, arbitrarily granular benchmarks for meaningful evaluation of LLM capabilities in RTL development workflows.

</details>


### [99] [AME-PIM: Can Memory be Your Next Tensor Accelerator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27808)
*Emanuele Venieri,Simone Manoni,Alberto Florian,Jaehyun Park,Kyomin Sohn,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PEP的执行模型，将RISC-V AME矩阵指令映射到HBM-PIM微内核，实现了无归约外积数据流，在Samsung Aquabolt-XL上单伪通道矩阵乘法达到14.9 GFLOP/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用HBM-PIM平台指令集有限且需要专用软件栈，本文旨在探索HBM-PIM是否能作为ISA级矩阵加速的后端，以RISC-V AME作为语义参考，解决数据移动开销问题。

Method: 提出PEP执行模型将AME元素级和矩阵指令映射到HBM-PIM微内核与内存操作；设计无归约外积数据流，在没有原生归约支持的情况下实现完全在内存内的累加；支持元素级运算、GEMV和GEMM的PIM模式端到端执行。

Result: 在Samsung Aquabolt-XL平台上，AME矩阵分块乘法在单个HBM伪通道上实现最高14.9 GFLOP/s（59.4 FLOP/周期）的性能，同时最小化主机参与和片外传输。

Conclusion: HBM-PIM能够有效作为ISA级矩阵加速的后端，通过创新的执行模型和数据流设计，在现有硬件限制下实现了高效的矩阵运算加速，为PIM技术在矩阵加速领域的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High Bandwidth Memory with Processing-in-Memory (HBM-PIM) offers an opportunity to reduce data movement by executing computation directly inside memory, but current commercial platforms expose limited instruction sets and require specialized software stacks. In this work, we investigate whether HBM-PIM can serve as a backend for ISA-level matrix acceleration, using the RISC-V Attached Matrix Extension (AME) as a semantic reference. We propose a PEP-based execution model that maps AME element-wise and matrix instructions to HBM-PIM micro-kernels and data instructions in memory operations. Differently from SoA HBM-PIM, we introduce a reduction-free outer-product dataflow that enables accumulation entirely within memory despite the lack of native reduction support. Our approach supports end-to-end execution of element-wise operations, GEMV, and GEMM in PIM mode, minimizing host involvement and off-chip transfers. An experimental evaluation on Samsung Aquabolt-XL shows that AME matrix tile multiplication achieves up to 14.9 GFLOP/s (59.4 FLOP/cycle) on a single HBM pseudo-channel.

</details>


### [100] [NeuroRing: Scaling Spiking Neural Networks via Multi-FPGA Bidirectional Ring Topologies and Stream-Dataflow Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28059)
*Muhammad Ihsan Al Hafiz,Artur Podobas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuroRing，一种基于FPGA的模块化、可扩展脉冲神经网络（SNN）加速器。它采用流数据流架构和双向环拓扑，支持单/多FPGA部署并与NEST模拟器集成。在皮层微回路和数独约束满足任务上，NeuroRing实现了快于实时的执行（RTF=0.83），并展现了良好的扩展性和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管脉冲神经网络（SNN）在能效方面具有巨大潜力，但大规模SNN的执行仍面临严峻挑战。稀疏的脉冲通信和同步操作往往会占据大量运行时间，成为性能瓶颈。现有的CPU、GPU、ASIC和FPGA解决方案在可编程性、效率和可扩展性之间存在权衡，缺乏一个既能保持可编程性又能实现高效扩展的统一平台。

Method: 提出了NeuroRing加速器，采用基于流数据流（stream-dataflow）的架构和双向环（bidirectional ring）拓扑结构。该设计通过高层次综合（HLS）在可编程FPGA上实现，支持模块化的单FPGA和多FPGA部署。为了确保兼容性，系统与现有的NEST模拟器工作流进行了集成。

Result: 在皮层微电路（cortical microcircuit）基准测试和数独约束满足工作负载上的评估表明：1）NeuroRing能够保持NEST参考模型的关键活动统计数据；2）在全规模皮层微电路上实现了快于实时的执行，实时因子（RTF）为0.83；3）表现出有效的强扩展性和弱扩展性；4）在两块可编程FPGA上提供了具有竞争力的能效。

Conclusion: NeuroRing成功填补了现有平台在可编程性、效率和可扩展性之间的空白，为神经科学模拟和更广泛的事件驱动应用提供了一个灵活、可扩展的高效硬件平台。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are a promising paradigm for energy-efficient event-driven computation, but large-scale SNN execution remains challenging because sparse spike communication and synchronization can dominate runtime. Existing solutions across CPU, GPU, ASIC, and FPGA platforms offer different trade-offs between programmability, efficiency, and scalability. To address this gap, we present NeuroRing, a modular and scalable SNN accelerator based on a stream-dataflow architecture and a bidirectional ring topology, implemented in High-Level Synthesis (HLS) on programmable FPGAs. NeuroRing supports modular single- and multi-FPGA deployment and is compatible with existing SNN workflows through integration with the NEST simulator. We evaluate NeuroRing on the cortical microcircuit benchmark and a Sudoku constraint-satisfaction workload. Results show that NeuroRing preserves the key activity statistics of the NEST reference model, achieves faster-than-real-time execution of the full-scale cortical microcircuit with a real-time factor (RTF) of 0.83, exhibits meaningful strong and weak scaling, and provides competitive energy efficiency on two programmable FPGAs. These results position NeuroRing as a flexible and scalable platform for both neuroscience simulation and broader event-driven applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [BLINC: Context-Specific Causal Learning for Automated RAN Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27084)
*Reshma Prasad,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLINC框架，结合LLM与贝叶斯网络进行RAN配置优化，在私有5G部署中实现了63.5%的吞吐量提升和19.7%的误块率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAN配置依赖大量人工，且现有数据驱动方法难以区分统计相关与因果关系，跨场景泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出BLINC框架，利用LLM辅助贝叶斯网络，将电信领域知识融入因果结构学习，并引入增量条件概率分布（CPD）更新机制以适应动态网络环境。

Result: 在私有5G部署中，通过联合优化功率控制和链路自适应参数，吞吐量提升63.5%，误块率降低19.7%，并提供可解释的因果结构及预测不确定性量化。

Conclusion: BLINC有效提升了RAN配置的自动化与智能化水平，具备良好的可解释性和场景适应能力，为动态网络环境下的持续优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Radio Access Network (RAN) configuration has traditionally required significant manual effort due to indirect causal dependencies between observable Key Performance Indicators (KPIs), and context-dependent characteristics, where the optimal configurations vary with network conditions. Although recent data-driven approaches improve parameter tuning, they remain limited in distinguishing causal direction from statistical correlation and in generalizing across diverse operating contexts.
  To address these challenges, we propose BLINC (Bayesian Large Language Model (LLM)-Driven Intelligent Network Configuration), an LLM-assisted Bayesian Network framework that integrates telecommunications domain knowledge into causal structure learning. Trained and validated on a private 5G deployment, our method achieves throughput improvement of 63.5% with 19.7% reduction on block error rate over data-only baselines through joint optimization of power control and link adaptation parameters. The framework provides interpretable causal structure, while also quantifying prediction uncertainty. We also demonstrate the ability of the Bayesian Network framework to adapt to different deployment scenarios and propose an incremental Conditional Probability Distribution (CPD) update mechanism with learning rate for continuous model adaptation as network conditions evolve.

</details>


### [102] [ReVo: A Cross-Layer Reliable Volumetric Videoconferencing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27441)
*Ankur Aditya,Diptyaroop Maji,Lingdong Wang,Bhavya Ramakrishna,Ramesh Sitaraman,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReVo系统，一种面向体积视频会议的抗丢包方案，通过跨层、模态感知的设计联合恢复RGB和深度内容，显著提升了视觉质量并减少了视频卡顿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的体积视频传输方案要么假设网络可靠，要么仅针对2D视频进行丢包恢复，无法有效应对体积视频会议中丢包导致的视觉质量下降、几何结构损坏和视频卡顿问题。因此，亟需一种能在实时约束下同时恢复RGB和深度内容的抗丢包系统。

Method: ReVo采用跨层、模态感知的设计：1）将体积视频解耦为RGB和深度流；2）在网络层使用前向纠错（FEC）选择性保护关键内容；3）使用后解码神经恢复模块重建受损的非关键帧。系统基于WebRTC端到端实现，支持传统和神经视频编解码器。

Result: 使用真实丢包轨迹的评估表明，ReVo将RGB（深度）内容的中位SSIM提升了最高32%（13%），并将视频卡顿减少了最高95.7%。

Conclusion: ReVo通过联合优化网络层保护与解码后神经恢复，有效解决了体积视频会议中的丢包问题，在桌面级硬件上满足实时性约束，显著提升了用户体验质量。

Abstract: Volumetric videoconferencing enables immersive six Degrees of Freedom interactions by jointly transmitting visual appearance and 3D geometry. However, delivering volumetric video over today's networks remains challenging due to high bandwidth demands, strict real-time latency constraints, and frequent packet loss. Packet loss not only degrades visual quality but also corrupts geometric structure, leading to severe artifacts and video freezes that significantly degrade Quality of Experience. Existing solutions either optimize volumetric videos assuming reliable networks or focus on loss recovery for 2D video, and are insufficient for volumetric videoconferencing. In this paper, we present ReVo, a loss-resilient volumetric videoconferencing system that jointly recovers RGB and depth content under packet loss while meeting real-time constraints on desktop-grade hardware. ReVo leverages the insight that effective recovery requires a cross-layer, modality-aware design. It decouples volumetric video into RGB and depth streams, selectively protects critical content using network-layer FEC, and reconstructs corrupted non-critical frames using a post-decode neural recovery module. ReVo is implemented end-to-end over WebRTC and supports both traditional and neural video codecs. Our evaluations using real-world loss traces show that ReVo improves median SSIM by up to 32% (resp. 13%) for RGB (resp. depth) content and reduces video freezes by up to 95.7% compared to existing techniques.

</details>


### [103] [Unified 5G-IoT Framework with CAMARA Gateways and SDN Federation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27589)
*Zihan Jia,Ze Wang,Chen Chen,Ziren Xiao,Fung Po Tso*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CAMARA开放网关和联合SDN架构的统一框架，旨在解决5G与IoT融合过程中的网络碎片化和异构性问题，并实现了5G对KNX设备的远程控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G与物联网的融合虽然能够构建全连接的智能环境，但面临着公私5G网络碎片化以及物联网网络异构性带来的互操作性挑战。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，利用CAMARA开放网关提供标准化的开放API以暴露网络能力，从而减少碎片化并简化互操作性；同时采用联合SDN架构以确保跨域控制的可扩展性。

Result: 成功展示了基于5G的KNX设备远程控制，将应用场景扩展至工业和楼宇自动化领域。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建安全、动态且支持下一代应用的“网络之网络”奠定了基础。

Abstract: The convergence of 5G and IoT enables fully connected, intelligent environments, but it faces challenges from the fragmentation of public/private 5G networks and the heterogeneity of IoT networks. We propose a unified framework using CAMARA open gateways, which provide standardized, open APIs to expose network capabilities, reducing fragmentation and simplifying interoperability, supported by a federated SDN architecture that ensures scalable cross-domain control. We further demonstrate 5G-based remote control of KNX devices, extending industrial and building automation. These contributions lay the foundation for a secure, dynamic "network of networks" supporting next-generation applications.

</details>


### [104] [Multi-Connectivity for UAVs: A Measurement Study of Integrating Cellular, Aerial Mesh, and LEO Satellite Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27640)
*Aygun Baltaci,Irshad A. Meer,Mustafa Ozger,Cicek Cavdar,Dominic Schupke*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过无人机飞行实验发现，在异构网络（空对空网格、蜂窝、卫星）中使用多路径传输（MPTCP）虽能维持连接，但链路间的RTT差异会导致严重的包乱序和缓冲问题，无法满足实时业务的时延约束，揭示了连通性连续性与服务连续性的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来的无人机系统需要整合空对空网格、地面蜂窝和低轨卫星等异构通信技术以提高鲁棒性和覆盖范围。多路径传输是聚合这些链路的自然机制，但其在高度异构环境下支持实时无人机服务的能力尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 基于真实的无人机飞行实验，在包含无人机到无人机空对空网格、私有蜂窝和低轨卫星（LEO）连接的集成网络环境中进行测量研究，使用多路径TCP（MPTCP）作为代表性的无损、有序多路径传输框架进行测试与分析。

Result: 多路径聚合可在严重链路中断情况下保持端到端连通性。然而，巨大的往返时延（RTT）差异加剧了包乱序，导致接收端大量缓冲和突发性交付；当链路容量不足时，发送端缓冲问题突出。这些效应导致实时流传输违反时延约束，即使在总容量充足的情况下也是如此。

Conclusion: 研究正式界定了连通性连续性与服务连续性的区别，并实证表明：在多技术无人机网络中，维持连通性是及时实时交付的必要条件但非充分条件。研究结果推动了显式考虑时延约束而非仅优化连通性的多路径设计。

Abstract: Future uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) systems increasingly combine heterogeneous communication technologies, such as low-latency aerial mesh, terrestrial cellular, and satellite links, to improve robustness and coverage. Multipath transport is a natural mechanism for aggregating these links, yet its ability to support real-time UAV services in highly heterogeneous environments remains insufficiently characterized. We present a measurement-driven study based on UAV flight experiments in an integrated network comprising UAV-to-UAV aerial mesh, private cellular, and low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite connectivity. Using Multipath TCP (MPTCP) as a representative lossless, in-order multipath transport framework, we find that aggregation can preserve end-to-end connectivity under severe link outages. However, large round-trip time (RTT) heterogeneity amplifies packet reordering, leading to substantial receiver-side buffering and bursty delivery. In addition, when the available links do not provide sufficient capacity for the offered load, pronounced sender-side buffering emerges. These effects cause real-time streaming to violate delay constraints, including cases where aggregate capacity is sufficient. To interpret these results, we formalize the distinction between connectivity continuity and service continuity and show empirically that maintaining connectivity is necessary but not sufficient for timely real-time delivery in multi-technology UAV networks. The findings motivate multipath designs that explicitly account for delay constraints, rather than optimizing for connectivity alone.

</details>


### [105] [LZn : Robust LoRa Frame Synchronization Under Frame Collisions and Ultra-Low SNR Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27672)
*José Álamos,Thomas C. Schmidt,Matthias Wählisch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LZn，一种基于频谱相交操作的低复杂度同步方案，显著提升了LoRa在低信噪比和高碰撞率场景下的帧检测与解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集的LoRa部署中，相同扩频因子之间的帧碰撞会导致传统接收机无法正确检测和解码帧。现有的改进方法在低信噪比（SNR）和高碰撞率环境下，其检测性能会急剧下降，迫切需要一种更鲁棒的同步机制。

Method: 提出了LZn同步方案，该方案由频谱相交操作（spectral intersection operation）驱动。这是一种低复杂度的方法，旨在实现鲁棒的帧同步，能够应对多数据包重叠或极低信噪比条件。

Result: LZn在检测灵敏度上提升了高达10dB，检测概率提升了高达1.54倍。在最具挑战性的单用户场景中，LZn的解码能力提升了3.46倍；在碰撞场景中，相较于次优方案（TnB）提升了1.22倍。

Conclusion: LZn方案显著改善了LoRa接收机的帧恢复能力，同时保持了与实时要求的兼容性，证明了其在提升LoRa网络性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: LoRa has become a widely adopted wireless modulation scheme in LPWANs due to its low cost, long range, and minimal transmission power. However, collisions between frames of the same spreading factor -- common in dense LoRa deployments -- prevent conventional LoRa receivers from detecting and correctly decoding frames. Recent work has introduced methods to improve recovery, yet their detection stage degrades sharply under low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and high collision rates. In this work, we introduce LZn, a low-complexity synchronization scheme driven by a spectral intersection operation. Our method enables robust frame synchronization even under multiple packet overlaps or extremely low SNR conditions. We evaluate LZn on simulations and three independent, real-world LoRa datasets. LZn improves detection sensitivity by up to 10dB and increases detection probability by up to 1.54x. In real-world datasets, LZn improves decoding by 3.46x in the most challenging single-user scenario and up to 1.22x in collision scenarios compared to the second best collision-tolerant scheme (TnB). These results demonstrate that LZn substantially improves the frame recovery of LoRa receivers, while remaining compatible with real-time requirements.

</details>


### [106] [Libra: Accelerating Socket I/O via Programmable Selective Data Copying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27686)
*Kairui Zhou,Shengkai Lin,Wei Zhang,Shizhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Libra框架，通过仅将元数据复制到用户空间、保留大容量负载在内核中转发，解决了L7代理内核-用户空间数据拷贝瓶颈，同时保持对未修改应用和标准socket API的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云原生系统中L7代理的性能日益受到内核-用户空间边界全量负载拷贝的瓶颈限制，现有解决方案通常需要牺牲兼容性、引入新API或依赖特定环境，而作者发现在传统OS抽象下完全消除拷贝同时保持标准socket语义是不可能的。

Method: 基于L7代理通常仅检查小量元数据（如HTTP头部）用于路由、而大量负载保持不变转发的观察，Libra利用eBPF识别协议特定的元数据边界，协调选择性拷贝和负载复用，仅将元数据复制到用户空间，大量负载保留在内核中转发，无需修改socket API。

Result: 在Linux上实现并使用未修改的Nginx和HAProxy评估，Libra将明文吞吐量提升高达4.2倍，P99尾延迟降低超过90%；在硬件卸载kTLS支持下，加密吞吐量提升2.0倍，尾延迟降低65%。

Conclusion: Libra通过选择性拷贝机制有效减少了内核-用户空间数据移动开销，在不牺牲兼容性和不修改应用的前提下显著提升了L7代理性能，为云原生系统提供了一种实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Layer-7 (L7) proxies are critical to modern cloud-native systems, yet their performance is increasingly bottlenecked by copying entire payloads across the kernel-user boundary. Existing approaches reduce this overhead but typically sacrifice compatibility with unmodified POSIX applications, introduce new APIs, or require specialized environments. We show that, under conventional OS abstractions, fully eliminating kernel-user copies while preserving standard socket semantics for unmodified proxies is fundamentally impossible. This leads to a practical insight: in common L7 workloads, proxies inspect only small metadata (e.g., HTTP headers) for routing, while forwarding the bulk payload unchanged. Based on this insight, we present Libra, an OS-level selective-copy framework that copies only metadata to the user space and retains the bulk payload in the kernel for forwarding, reducing data movement without breaking compatibility. Libra uses eBPF to identify protocol-specific metadata boundaries and coordinate selective copy and payload reuse across receive and transmit paths, all without modifying the socket API. Implemented in Linux and evaluated with unmodified Nginx and HAProxy, Libra improves plaintext throughput by up to 4.2x and reduces P99 tail latency by over 90%. With hardware-offloaded kTLS, it boosts encrypted throughput by 2.0x and cuts tail latency by 65%.

</details>


### [107] [NetSatBench: A Distributed LEO Constellation Emulator with an SRv6 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27854)
*Andrea Detti,Shahram Dadras,Giuseppe Tropea*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetSatBench是一个用于大规模LEO卫星系统的分布式仿真平台，通过容器化架构和声明式工作流支持通信协议与应用负载的端到端评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库导向工具要求用户编写控制程序，使用门槛高；缺乏针对大规模LEO卫星系统的端到端仿真平台，难以有效评估时变网络动态下的协议性能与切换策略。

Method: 采用分布式架构，将卫星、网关和用户终端实现为分布在裸机或虚拟机集群中的Linux容器；通过二层VXLAN-overlay实现链路仿真；使用Etcd键值存储维护系统状态，通过epoch文件更新链路和任务变更；采用基于JSON场景文件和命令行接口的高级声明式工作流；通过外部插件将物理层和路由建模与仿真器核心解耦，内置支持IPv4/IPv6路由（包括IS-IS和理想时变路由）。

Result: 通过基于SRv6的LEO架构案例研究验证了平台能力，该架构在不同切换策略下管理用户与网关之间的数据隧道；实验表明平台能够支持时变LEO动态下的协议级实验，并揭示了联合考虑服务用户和网关的卫星的端到端切换策略的重要性。

Conclusion: NetSatBench为大规模LEO卫星系统提供了可扩展、易用的仿真环境，能够有效支持协议级实验并揭示端到端切换策略的关键作用，为LEO网络架构设计与评估提供了重要工具。

Abstract: NetSatBench is a distributed emulation platform for evaluating communication protocols and application workloads over large-scale LEO satellite systems. Satellites, gateways, and user terminals are implemented as Linux containers distributed across a cluster of bare-metal or virtual machines, while emulated links are realized through a Layer-2 VXLAN overlay. The system state is maintained in an Etcd key-value store and updated through epoch files, which propagate link and task changes to local control agents running inside the emulated nodes. In contrast to library-oriented tools that require users to write control programs, NetSatBench adopts a higher-level declarative workflow based on JSON "scenario files" and a command-line interface. The platform decouples physical-layer and routing modeling from the emulator core through external plug-ins, while providing built-in support for IPv4 and IPv6 routing, including IS-IS and ideal time-varying routing. Rather than focusing on emulator micro-performance alone, we illustrate what NetSatBench enables through an SRv6-based LEO architecture in which control procedures manage data tunnels between users and gateways under different handover policies. This case study shows how NetSatBench can support protocol-level experimentation under time-varying LEO dynamics and highlights the importance of end-to-end handover strategies that jointly account for the satellites serving both the user and the gateway.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [MARS: Efficient, Adaptive Co-Scheduling for Heterogeneous Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26963)
*Yifei Wang,Hancheng Ye,Yechen Xu,Cong Guo,Chiyue Wei,Qinsi Wang,Dongting Li,Tingjun Chen,Hai "Helen" Li,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARS系统，通过全局协调GPU-CPU异构资源，解决了自主Agent执行中的资源调度问题，实现了端到端延迟降低5.94倍和任务完成时间加速1.87倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLMs)正从独立文本生成器转变为自主Agent的执行核心。这种转变带来了两个关键挑战：(1)时间维度上从单轮推理转向多轮LLM-工具循环；(2)空间维度上从聊天级别的纯GPU执行转向仓库级别的GPU-CPU协同执行。因此，协调Agent执行的异构资源需求成为关键系统挑战。

Method: 设计并实现了MARS系统，包含两个核心组件：(1)通过统一信息流建立GPU推理和CPU工具执行的全面可见性，外部控制平面将准入与执行解耦以防止异构资源过度订阅；(2)内部Agent中心调度器通过优先处理延迟敏感的连续任务并自适应保留KV缓存状态来最小化端到端关键路径。

Result: 评估结果显示：MARS将端到端延迟降低高达5.94倍，同时保持接近最大的系统吞吐量。在OpenHands编程Agent框架中集成MARS后，端到端任务完成时间加速高达1.87倍。

Conclusion: MARS成功解决了自主Agent执行中的异构资源协调问题，证明了全局协调GPU-CPU资源、解耦准入与执行、以及Agent中心调度的有效性，为实际应用中的Agent系统提供了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as the execution core of autonomous agents rather than as standalone text generators. Agentic workloads induce a temporal shift from single-turn inference to multi-turn LLM-tool loops, and a spatial shift from chat-scale, GPU-only execution to repository-scale, GPU-CPU co-located execution. Consequently, coordinating heterogeneous resource demands of agentic execution has emerged as a critical system challenge.
  We design and implement MARS, an efficient and adaptive co-scheduling system that globally coordinates heterogeneous agentic workloads under coupled GPU-CPU resource pressure. By establishing holistic visibility across GPU inference and CPU tool execution via a unified information stream, an external control plane in MARS decouples admission from execution to prevent heterogeneous resource oversubscription. An internal agent-centric scheduler further minimizes the end-to-end critical path by prioritizing latency-sensitive continuations and adaptively retaining KV cache state only when warm resumption yields a latency benefit. Our evaluations show that MARS reduces end-to-end latency by up to 5.94x while maintaining nearly maximal system throughput. We further integrate MARS as the serving backend for the OpenHands coding agent framework, demonstrating its real-world effectiveness by accelerating end-to-end task completion time by up to 1.87x.
  Our source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [109] [Affinity Tailor: Dynamic Locality-Aware Scheduling at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27915)
*Jin Xin Ng,Ori Livneh,Richard O'Grady,Josh Don,Peng Ding,Samuel Grossman,Luis Otero,Chris Kennelly,David Lo,Carlos Villavieja*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出了Affinity Tailor系统，通过用户空间引导内核调度，将按需求大小、拓扑紧凑的CPU集合作为亲和性提示而非硬分区，在保持CPU利用率的同时显著提升了缓存和分支预测等微架构层面的局部性，在Google生产环境中实现了显著的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多核系统中，Linux CFS等调度器通过负载均衡保持CPU忙碌，导致工作负载在多个核心间频繁迁移，削弱了缓存、分支预测器和预取器的局部性，尤其在chiplet架构下跨LLC边界的干扰更为严重。而严格的CPU分区虽然能保持局部性，但在工作负载未充分利用预留CPU时会导致容量闲置。

Method: 设计了一种用户空间引导的内核调度系统。用户空间控制器在线估算每个工作负载的CPU需求，分配大小匹配且拓扑紧凑的优选CPU集合（尽量与其他负载分离且跨越最少LLC域），内核将该集合作为亲和性提示引导线程执行，同时在必要时允许在其他CPU上执行以保持利用率。

Result: 在Google部署后，相较于Linux CFS，在chiplet系统上实现了12%的几何平均每CPU吞吐量提升，非chiplet系统上提升3%；更快的执行减少了内存驻留，带来每GB 3-7%的吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: 研究表明空间局部性应作为调度器的一等目标，即使可能牺牲部分工作保持特性，未来调度器设计应优先考虑局部性优化。

Abstract: Modern large multicore systems often run multiple workloads that share CPUs under schedulers such as Linux CFS. To keep CPUs busy, these schedulers load-balance runnable work, causing each workload to execute on many cores. This weakens locality at the microarchitectural level: workloads lose reuse in caches, branch predictors, and prefetchers, and interfere more with one another - especially on chiplet-based systems, where spreading execution across cores also spreads it across LLC boundaries. A natural alternative is strict CPU partitioning, but hard partitions leave capacity idle when workloads do not fully use their reserved CPUs.
  We present Affinity Tailor, a userspace-guided kernel scheduling system built on a key insight: the kernel can preserve locality for workloads that share CPUs by treating demand-sized, topologically compact CPU sets as affinity hints rather than hard partitions. A userspace controller estimates each workload's CPU demand online and assigns a preferred CPU set sized to that demand, chosen to be as disjoint as possible from other workloads while spanning as few LLC domains as possible. The kernel then uses this set as an affinity hint, steering threads toward those CPUs while still allowing execution elsewhere when needed to preserve utilization. Deployed at Google, Affinity Tailor delivers geometric-mean per-CPU throughput gains of 12% on chiplet-based systems and 3% on non-chiplet systems over Linux CFS. Furthermore, faster execution reduces memory residency, yielding per-GB throughput gains of 3-7%. Our findings suggest that future schedulers should treat spatial locality as a first-class objective, even at the expense of work-conservation.

</details>


### [110] [Crab: A Semantics-Aware Checkpoint/Restore Runtime for Agent Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28138)
*Tianyuan Wu,Chaokun Chang,Lunxi Cao,Wei Gao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Crab是一个透明的主机端运行时系统，通过eBPF检测器识别Agent回合的OS可见效应，智能决策检查点粒度，在保持100%恢复正确性的同时，将检查点流量降低高达87%，性能开销仅1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主Agent通过沙箱容器和微虚拟机执行，其状态跨越文件系统、进程和运行时构件。检查点与恢复(C/R)对于容错、spot执行、强化学习rollout分支和安全回滚至关重要。现有方法陷入两个极端：应用级恢复保留聊天历史但遗漏OS副作用；全量逐回合检查点虽然正确但在密集共置环境下代价过高。根本原因是Agent-OS语义鸿沟：Agent框架看到工具调用但看不到其OS效应；OS看到状态变化但缺乏回合级上下文判断恢复相关性。该鸿沟掩盖了大量稀疏性：超过75%的Agent回合不产生恢复相关状态，因此大多数检查点是不必要的。

Method: Crab是一个透明的主机端运行时，在不修改Agent或C/R后端的情况下弥合语义鸿沟。其核心组件包括：基于eBPF的检查器对每个回合的OS可见效应进行分类以决定检查点粒度；协调器将检查点与回合边界对齐，并将C/R与LLM等待时间重叠；主机范围引擎在共置沙箱间调度检查点流量。

Result: 在Shell密集型和代码修复工作负载上，Crab将恢复正确率从8%（仅聊天历史）提升至100%，将检查点流量减少高达87%，并且在执行时间上保持与无故障执行仅1.9%的差距。

Conclusion: Crab成功解决了Agent沙箱检查点与恢复中的Agent-OS语义鸿沟问题，通过智能识别恢复相关状态并利用稀疏性，在保证恢复正确性的同时大幅降低了系统开销，为自主Agent系统的容错、资源优化和安全回滚提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agents act through sandboxed containers and microVMs whose state spans filesystems, processes, and runtime artifacts. Checkpoint and restore (C/R) of this state is needed for fault tolerance, spot execution, RL rollout branching, and safe rollback-yet existing approaches fall into two extremes: application-level recovery preserves chat history but misses OS-side effects, while full per-turn checkpointing is correct but too expensive under dense co-location. The root cause is an agent-OS semantic gap: agent frameworks see tool calls but not their OS effects; the OS sees state changes but lacks turn-level context to judge recovery relevance. This gap hides massive sparsity: over 75% of agent turns produce no recovery-relevant state, so most checkpoints are unnecessary. Crab (Checkpoint-and-Restore for Agent SandBoxes) is a transparent host-side runtime that bridges this gap without modifying agents or C/R backends. An eBPF-based inspector classifies each turn's OS-visible effects to decide checkpoint granularity; a coordinator aligns checkpoints with turn boundaries and overlaps C/R with LLM wait time; and a host-scoped engine schedules checkpoint traffic across co-located sandboxes. On shell-intensive and code-repair workloads, Crab raises recovery correctness from 8% (chat-only) to 100%, cuts checkpoint traffic by up to 87%, and stays within 1.9% of fault-free execution time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Compositional Meta-Learning for Mitigating Task Heterogeneity in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26999)
*Beomchul Park,Minsu Koh,Heejo Kong,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LAM-PINN框架，通过任务聚类与模块化路由解决参数化偏微分方程族中PINNs跨任务迁移的负迁移问题，在减少90%训练迭代的同时实现近20倍的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数化偏微分方程族中，为每个任务单独训练物理信息神经网络(PINNs)计算代价高昂，而现有跨任务迁移方法在任务异质性、特征稀缺的坐标输入及训练任务有限的情况下容易产生负迁移，且依赖单一全局初始化难以适应多样化的任务需求。

Method: 提出学习亲和力自适应模块化物理信息神经网络(LAM-PINN)：利用PDE参数与短暂迁移会话中的学习亲和力指标构建任务表示并进行聚类；将模型分解为集群专用子网络与共享元网络；通过学习路由权重选择性复用模块，替代单一全局初始化策略。

Result: 在三个PDE基准测试中，LAM-PINN在仅使用传统PINNs 10%训练迭代的情况下，对未见任务的均方误差(MSE)平均降低19.7倍。

Conclusion: LAM-PINN证明了模块化组合与自适应路由在参数化PDE族任务迁移中的有效性，能够在资源受限的工程场景中实现对新配置的高效泛化。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) approximate solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into the loss function. In parameterized PDE families, variations in coefficients or boundary/initial conditions define distinct tasks. This makes training individual PINNs for each task computationally prohibitive, while cross-task transfer can be sensitive to task heterogeneity. While meta-learning can reduce retraining cost, existing methods often rely on a single global initialization and may suffer from negative transfer, particularly under feature-scarce coordinate inputs and limited training-task availability. We propose the Learning-Affinity Adaptive Modular Physics-Informed Neural Network (LAM-PINN), a compositional framework that leverages task-specific learning dynamics. LAM-PINN combines PDE parameters with learning-affinity metrics from brief transfer sessions to construct a task representation and cluster tasks even with coordinate-only inputs. It decomposes the model into cluster-specialized subnetworks and a shared meta network, and learns routing weights to selectively reuse modules instead of relying on a single global initialization. Across three PDE benchmarks, LAM-PINN achieves an average 19.7-fold reduction in mean squared error (MSE) on unseen tasks using only 10% of the training iterations required by conventional PINNs. These results indicate its effectiveness for generalization to unseen configurations within bounded design spaces of parameterized PDE families in resource-constrained engineering settings.

</details>


### [112] [Binary Spiking Neural Networks as Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27007)
*Aditya Kar,Emiliano Lorini,Timothée Masquelier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将二元脉冲神经网络（BSNN）的脉冲活动建模为二元因果模型，利用SAT和SMT求解器计算基于逻辑的溯因解释，从而提供具有形式化保证的特征相关性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释AI方法（如SHAP）无法保证解释中的特征完全相关，可能包含无关特征。因此，需要一种能提供形式化保证、确保解释中不含无关特征的因果分析方法来解释BSNN的行为。

Method: 首先形式化定义二元脉冲神经网络（BSNN），将其脉冲活动表示为二元因果模型。基于该因果表示，利用SAT（可满足性）求解器和SMT（可满足性模理论）求解器计算溯因解释，从而解释网络输出。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上训练BSNN并应用该方法，成功找到了基于像素级特征的溯因解释。与SHAP方法的对比实验表明，本方法能保证解释中不包含完全无关的特征，而SHAP无法提供此保证。

Conclusion: 通过将BSNN的脉冲活动建模为二元因果模型并利用逻辑求解器，可以实现具有形式化保证的网络解释，确保解释中的特征相关性，为可解释AI领域提供了一种可靠的新方法。

Abstract: We provide a causal analysis of Binary Spiking Neural Networks (BSNNs) to explain their behavior. We formally define a BSNN and represent its spiking activity as a binary causal model. Thanks to this causal representation, we are able to explain the output of the network by leveraging logic-based methods. In particular, we show that we can successfully use a SAT as well as a SMT solver to compute abductive explanations from this binary causal model. To illustrate our approach, we trained the BSNN on the standard MNIST dataset and applied our SAT-based and SMT-based methods to finding abductive explanations of the network's classifications based on pixel-level features. We also compared the found explanations against SHAP, a popular method used in the area of explainable AI. We show that, unlike SHAP, our approach guarantees that a found explanation does not contain completely irrelevant features.

</details>


### [113] [When Roles Fail: Epistemic Constraints on Advocate Role Fidelity in LLM-Based Political Statement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27228)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对多智能体LLM系统中的角色保真度进行系统性实证测试，发现模型难以始终维持分配的对抗性角色，存在“认知角色覆盖”机制，且不同模型表现差异显著，直接影响系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM管道在民主话语分析中日益普及，其核心假设是模型能可靠地维持被分配的角色。然而这一假设缺乏系统性实证验证，若模型角色发生漂移，系统将无法提供设计时所预期的多视角评估，因此亟需检验该假设的有效性。

Method: 基于TRUST管道，开发不依赖表面词汇的认知立场分类器，在60条政治声明（英德各30条）上测量角色保真度。采用四项指标：角色漂移指数(RDI)、期望漂移距离(EDD)、方向性漂移指数(DDI)和基于熵的角色稳定性(ERS)。对比了不同模型、语言和事实核查提供商的影响。

Result: 发现两种失效模式（认知底线效应和角色-先验冲突）均源于同一机制——认知角色覆盖(ERO)。Mistral Large角色保真度(67%)显著优于Claude Sonnet(39%)，两者失效模式定性不同；角色保真度具有语言鲁棒性；事实核查提供商选择非中立：Perplexity显著降低Claude在德语上的角色保真度(Delta=-15pp, p=0.007)。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM验证系统若缺乏角色保真度测量，可能系统性地错误呈现其设计初衷所要求的认知多样性，导致输出结果存在偏差，因此在部署此类系统时必须纳入角色保真度评估。

Abstract: Democratic discourse analysis systems increasingly rely on multi-agent LLM pipelines in which distinct evaluator models are assigned adversarial roles to generate structured, multi-perspective assessments of political statements. A core assumption is that models will reliably maintain their assigned roles. This paper provides the first systematic empirical test of that assumption using the TRUST pipeline. We develop an epistemic stance classifier that identifies advocate roles from reasoning text without relying on surface vocabulary, and measure role fidelity across 60 political statements (30 English, 30 German) using four metrics: Role Drift Index (RDI), Expected Drift Distance (EDD), Directional Drift Index (DDI), and Entropy-based Role Stability (ERS). We identify two failure modes - the Epistemic Floor Effect (fact-check results create an absolute lower bound below which the legitimizing role cannot be maintained) and Role-Prior Conflict (training-time knowledge overrides role instructions for factually unambiguous statements) - as manifestations of a single mechanism: Epistemic Role Override (ERO). Model choice significantly affects role fidelity: Mistral Large outperforms Claude Sonnet by 28pp (67% vs. 39%) and exhibits a qualitatively different failure mode - role abandonment without polarity reversal - compared to Claude's active switch to the opposing stance. Role fidelity is language-robust. Fact-check provider choice is not universally neutral: Perplexity significantly reduces Claude's role fidelity on German statements (Delta = -15pp, p = 0.007) while leaving Mistral unaffected. These findings have direct implications for multi-agent LLM validation: a system validated without role fidelity measurement may systematically misrepresent the epistemic diversity it was designed to provide.

</details>


### [114] [When Your LLM Reaches End-of-Life: A Framework for Confident Model Migration in Production Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27082)
*Emma Casey,David Roberts,David Sim,Ian Beaver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于贝叶斯统计的框架，用于在生产环境中迁移达到生命周期终点的大语言模型（LLM），通过校准自动评估指标与人工判断，实现了在有限人工数据下的可靠模型替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生态系统快速演进，生产系统中的底层模型面临生命周期终结或需要替换的问题，而传统评估方法在模型比较中往往需要大量人工标注，效率低下且成本高昂，缺乏一种既能保证质量又能提高评估效率的原则性方法。

Method: 提出了一种贝叶斯统计方法，将自动评估指标与人工判断进行校准，从而在有限的人工评估数据下实现置信度较高的模型比较和选择，构建了一套可重现的模型迁移方法论。

Result: 在一个服务六大全球区域、月交互量达530万次的商业问答系统上验证了该框架的有效性，成功评估了正确性、拒绝行为和风格遵从性，并识别出了合适的替代模型。

Conclusion: 该框架广泛适用于任何部署LLM产品的企业，提供了一种原则性强、可重现的模型迁移方法，在保证质量的同时提高了评估效率，对于管理多模型、多区域、多场景AI服务组合的组织具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present a framework for migrating production Large Language Model (LLM) based systems when the underlying model reaches end-of-life or requires replacement. The key contribution is a Bayesian statistical approach that calibrates automated evaluation metrics against human judgments, enabling confident model comparison even with limited manual evaluation data. We demonstrate this framework on a commercial question-answering system serving 5.3M monthly interactions across six global regions; evaluating correctness, refusal behavior, and stylistic adherence to successfully identify suitable replacement models. The framework is broadly applicable to any enterprise deploying LLM-based products, providing a principled, reproducible methodology for model migration that balances quality assurance with evaluation efficiency. This is a capability increasingly essential as the LLM ecosystem continues to evolve rapidly and organizations manage portfolios of AI-powered services across multiple models, regions, and use cases.

</details>


### [115] [Reinforced Agent: Inference-Time Feedback for Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27233)
*Anh Ta,Junjie Zhu,Shahin Shayandeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将评估嵌入执行循环的审查智能体架构，通过Helpfulness-Harmfulness指标量化纠错与引入新错误的权衡，在BFCL和Tau2-Bench上实现+5.5%至+7.1%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用智能体的评估是事后进行的，无法在执行过程中实时纠错，只能通过提示调优或重新训练解决问题。作者希望将评估移至推理时的执行循环中，实现主动评估和错误缓解。

Method: 提出双智能体架构：主执行智能体负责工具调用，辅助审查智能体在执行前评估临时工具调用。引入Helpfulness-Harmfulness指标量化权衡：帮助性衡量被纠正的基础错误百分比，危害性衡量被反馈降级的正确响应百分比。使用GEPA进行自动化提示优化。

Result: 在BFCL（单轮）上无关性检测提升+5.5%，在Tau2-Bench（多轮状态场景）上提升+7.1%。推理模型o3-mini实现3:1的收益风险比，GPT-4o为2.1:1。自动化提示优化额外提升+1.5-2.8%。

Conclusion: 分离执行和审查具有核心优势：审查智能体可通过模型选择和提示优化系统性改进，无需重新训练基础智能体。审查模型的选择至关重要，推理模型表现更优。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents are evaluated on tool selection, parameter accuracy, and scope recognition, yet LLM trajectory assessments remain inherently post-hoc. Disconnected from the active execution loop, such assessments identify errors that are usually addressed through prompt-tuning or retraining, and fundamentally cannot course-correct the agent in real time. To close this gap, we move evaluation into the execution loop at inference time: a specialized reviewer agent evaluates provisional tool calls prior to execution, shifting the paradigm from post-hoc recovery to proactive evaluation and error mitigation.
  In practice, this architecture establishes a clear separation of concerns between the primary execution agent and a secondary review agent. As with any multi-agent system, the reviewer can introduce new errors while correcting others, yet no prior work to our knowledge has systematically measured this tradeoff. To quantify this tradeoff, we introduce Helpfulness-Harmfulness metrics: helpfulness measures the percentage of base agent errors that feedback corrects; harmfulness measures the percentage of correct responses that feedback degrades. These metrics directly inform reviewer design by revealing whether a given model or prompt provides net positive value.
  We evaluate our approach on BFCL (single-turn) and Tau2-Bench (multi-turn stateful scenarios), achieving +5.5% on irrelevance detection and +7.1% on multi-turn tasks. Our metrics reveal that reviewer model choice is critical: the reasoning model o3-mini achieves a 3:1 benefit-to-risk ratio versus 2.1:1 for GPT-4o. Automated prompt optimization via GEPA provides an additional +1.5-2.8%. Together, these results demonstrate a core advantage of separating execution and review: the reviewer can be systematically improved through model selection and prompt optimization, without retraining the base agent.

</details>


### [116] [End-to-end autonomous scientific discovery on a real optical platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27092)
*Shuxing Yang,Fujia Chen,Rui Zhao,Junyao Wu,Yize Wang,Haiyao Luo,Ning Han,Qiaolu Chen,Yuze Hu,Wenhao Li,Mingzhu Li,Hongsheng Chen,Yihao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了Qiushi Discovery Engine，这是首个在真实物理系统（光学平台）上实现端到端自主科学发现的大语言模型智能体系统。它不仅复现了已知实验，还首次观测到了一类相干阶结构，并自主提出并实验验证了一种新的物理机制——光学双线性相互作用，标志着AI智能体在研究级自主发现领域的里程碑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLM）智能体已开始辅助科研工作，但目前尚未有系统在真实物理体系中实现端到端的自主科学发现并获得有实验证据支持的非平凡结果。为了突破这一局限，作者旨在构建一个能够自主进行完整科研流程（包括假设提出、实验设计、数据分析与修正）的AI智能体系统，以探索AI在科学研究中的自主发现能力。

Method: 作者提出了Qiushi Discovery Engine，这是一个基于LLM的智能体系统。其核心架构包括：非线性研究阶段、Meta-Trace记忆机制以及双层架构，旨在长期的探索过程中（涉及数千次LLM推理、测量与修正动作）维持自适应且稳定的研究轨迹。该系统部署在真实的光学实验平台上进行验证。

Result: Qiushi Engine成功实现了三个层次的发现：1) 在非原始平台上自主复现了已发表的传输矩阵实验；2) 将抽象的相干阶理论转化为实验可观测值，首次观测到了此类相干阶结构；3) 在一项大规模开放式研究中（消耗1.459亿tokens，执行3242次LLM调用与1242次工具调用），自主提出并实验验证了光学双线性相互作用——一种与Transformer注意力核心操作结构类似的物理机制，该机制为高速、节能的成对计算光学硬件提供了新路径。

Conclusion: 这是首个AI智能体系统自主识别并实验验证非平凡、未曾报道的物理机制的演示，证明了AI智能体具备进行端到端自主科学研究的能力。这一成果标志着研究级自主智能体发展的重要里程碑，展示了AI驱动科学发现的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Scientific research has long been human-led, driving new knowledge and transformative technologies through the continual revision of questions, methods and claims as evidence accumulates. Although large language model (LLM)-based agents are beginning to move beyond assisting predefined research workflows, none has yet demonstrated end-to-end autonomous discovery in a real physical system that produces a nontrivial result supported by experimental evidence. Here we introduce Qiushi Discovery Engine, an LLM-based agentic system for end-to-end autonomous scientific discovery on a real optical platform. Qiushi Engine combines nonlinear research phases, Meta-Trace memory and a dual-layer architecture to maintain adaptive and stable research trajectories across long-horizon investigations involving thousands of LLM-mediated reasoning, measurement and revision actions. It autonomously reproduces a published transmission-matrix experiment on a non-original platform and converts an abstract coherence-order theory into experimental observables, providing, to our knowledge, the first observation of this class of coherence-order structure. More importantly, in an open-ended study involving 145.9 million tokens, 3,242 LLM calls, 1,242 tool calls, 163 research notes and 44 scripts, Qiushi Engine proposes and experimentally validates optical bilinear interaction, a physical mechanism structurally analogous to a core operation in Transformer attention. This AI-discovered mechanism suggests a route towards high-speed, energy-efficient optical hardware for pairwise computation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI agentic system autonomously identifying and experimentally validating a nontrivial, previously unreported physical mechanism, marking a milestone for research-level autonomous agents.

</details>


### [117] [Autonomous Traffic Signal Optimization Using Digital Twin and Agentic AI for Real-Time Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27753)
*Salman Jan,Toqeer Ali Syed,Shahid Kamal,Qamar Wali,Ali Akarma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数字孪生和智能体AI的交通灯优化框架，通过感知-概念化-行动三层架构实现实时自主决策，在减少等待时间和提升交通流效率方面优于固定配时和强化学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统交通信号控制方法（如固定配时）无法适应实时交通变化的问题，并克服现有智能方法（如强化学习）在实时决策和系统集成方面的局限性，研究旨在建立一个能够实时感知、模拟并自主优化交通信号的智能框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于数字孪生的三层架构系统：1）感知层利用物理传感器和边缘计算获取实时交通数据；2）概念化层使用LangChain处理数据并在数字孪生体中模拟交通流；3）行动层通过模型上下文协议（MCP）和交通管理API执行优化的信号控制算法。系统根据交通拥堵、行程延误和交通模式自动控制交通灯。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架有效减少了交通灯处的等待时间，并显著提升了整体交通流的运行效率。与固定配时和基于强化学习的基线方法相比，该框架表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: 基于数字孪生和智能体AI的交通灯优化框架能够实现高效的实时自主决策，在交通管理领域具有重要的应用价值，为智能交通系统的发展提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: This article outlines a new framework of traffic light optimization through a digital twin of the transport infrastructure, managed by agentic AI to ensure real-time autonomous decisions. The framework relies on physical sensors and edge computing to measure real-time traffic information and simulate traffic flow in a constantly updated digital twin. The traffic light is automatically controlled through the digital twin according to traffic congestion, travel delay and traffic patterns. This approach is implemented as a three-layer system: perception, conceptualization and action. The perception layer receives data on physical systems; the conceptualization layer uses LangChain to process the data; and the action layer links to the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and traffic management APIs to implement optimised traffic signal control algorithms. The results show that the framework minimizes waiting time at traffic lights and positively affects the effectiveness of the entire traffic flow, which is better than the fixed-time and reinforcement learning-based baselines.

</details>


### [118] [Think it, Run it: Autonomous ML pipeline generation via self-healing multi-agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27096)
*Adela Bara,Gabriela Dobrita,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个五代理统一架构，通过集成代码增强RAG、可解释推荐、自愈执行和自适应学习，实现了从数据集和自然语言目标到端到端机器学习流程生成的自动化，成功率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统机器学习流程构建效率低、鲁棒性差和可解释性不足的问题，通过自动化手段从数据集和自然语言目标生成端到端机器学习流程，以提高整体开发效率和质量。

Method: 提出了一个包含五个代理的系统架构，分别负责性能分析、意图解析、微服务推荐、有向无环图(DAG)构建和执行。关键技术包括：用于微服务理解的代码增强检索增强生成(RAG)、结合多种标准的可解释混合推荐器、基于大语言模型(LLM)错误解释的自愈机制，以及从执行历史中进行自适应学习。

Result: 在150个不同场景的机器学习任务上进行了评估。系统实现了84.7%的端到端流程成功率，优于基线方法；通过自愈机制展示了改进的鲁棒性，并相比手动构建显著减少了工作流开发时间。

Conclusion: 该研究在一个单一架构内创新性地整合了代码增强RAG、可解释推荐、自愈执行和自适应学习，证明了紧密耦合的智能组件能够优于孤立的解决方案，为自动化机器学习流程生成提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The purpose of our paper is to develop a unified multi-agent architecture that automates end-to-end machine learning (ML) pipeline generation from datasets and natural-language (NL) goals, improving efficiency, robustness and explainability. A five-agent system is proposed to handle profiling, intent parsing, microservice recommendation, Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) construction and execution. It integrates code-grounded Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for microservice understanding, an explainable hybrid recommender combining multiple criteria, a self-healing mechanism using Large Language Model (LLM)-based error interpretation and adaptive learning from execution history. The approach is evaluated on 150 ML tasks across diverse scenarios. The system achieves an 84.7% end-to-end pipeline success rate, outperforming baseline methods. It demonstrates improved robustness through self-healing and reduces workflow development time compared to manual construction. The study introduces a novel integration of code-grounded RAG, explainable recommendation, self-healing execution and adaptive learning within a single architecture, showing that tightly coupled intelligent components can outperform isolated solutions.

</details>


### [119] [ObjectGraph: From Document Injection to Knowledge Traversal -- A Native File Format for the Agentic Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27820)
*Mohit Dubey,Open Gigantic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有文档格式不适应LLM代理检索式阅读的问题，提出OBJECTGRAPH (.og)格式，将文档重构为类型化的有向知识图谱，在保持任务准确率的同时实现高达95.3%的token削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档格式均面向人类线性阅读设计，而自主LLM代理的阅读方式是检索而非线性阅读。这种根本性错配导致代理被迫将整个文档注入上下文窗口，浪费token处理无关内容，在多轮循环中累积状态，并在不同代理角色间无差别广播信息。作者认为这不是提示工程、检索或压缩问题，而是格式问题。

Method: 提出OBJECTGRAPH (.og)文件格式，将文档重新定义为可遍历的类型化有向知识图谱而非待注入的字符串。该格式是Markdown的严格超集（每个.md文件都是有效的.og文件），仅需双原语查询协议，无需额外基础设施，人与代理均可直接阅读。同时引入渐进式披露模型、角色范围访问协议和可执行断言节点作为原生格式原语，形式化定义文档消费问题并证明OBJECTGRAPH满足六个结构性属性。

Result: 在五个文档类别和八种代理任务类型的实证评估中，OBJECTGRAPH实现了高达95.3%的token削减，且任务准确率无统计学显著下降(p > 0.05)。在保留文档基准测试中，转译器保真度达到98.7%的内容保留率。

Conclusion: OBJECTGRAPH成功解决了文档消费问题，通过将文档格式从线性文本重构为可遍历的知识图谱，显著提升了LLM代理的信息获取效率，证明了格式层面的创新可以有效解决代理与文档之间的根本性错配问题。

Abstract: Every document format in existence was designed for a human reader moving linearly through text. Autonomous LLM agents do not read - they retrieve. This fundamental mismatch forces agents to inject entire documents into their context window, wasting tokens on irrelevant content, compounding state across multi-turn loops, and broadcasting information indiscriminately across agent roles. We argue this is not a prompt engineering problem, not a retrieval problem, and not a compression problem: it is a format problem.
  We introduce OBJECTGRAPH (.og), a file format that reconceives the document as a typed, directed knowledge graph to be traversed rather than a string to be injected. OBJECTGRAPH is a strict superset of Markdown - every .md file is a valid .og file - requires no infrastructure beyond a two-primitive query protocol, and is readable by both humans and agents without tooling.
  We formalize the Document Consumption Problem, characterise six structural properties no existing format satisfies simultaneously, and prove OBJECTGRAPH satisfies all six. We further introduce the Progressive Disclosure Model, the Role-Scoped Access Protocol, and Executable Assertion Nodes as native format primitives. Empirical evaluation across five document classes and eight agent task types demonstrates up to 95.3 percent token reduction with no statistically significant degradation in task accuracy (p > 0.05). Transpiler fidelity reaches 98.7 percent content preservation on a held-out document benchmark.

</details>


### [120] [Unsupervised Electrofacies Classification and Porosity Characterization in the Offshore Keta Basin Using Wireline Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27126)
*Hamdiya Adams,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Daniel Kwadwo Asiedu,Bruce Kofi Banoeng-Yakubo,Marcellin Atemkeng,Thomas Armah,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis,Ezekiel Nii Noye Nortey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种无监督机器学习工作流程，仅利用测井数据在缺少岩心数据的加纳Keta盆地进行了电相分析，成功识别出四种电相类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对海上Keta盆地等勘探初期区域岩心数据匮乏的问题，旨在开发一种不依赖岩心数据、仅基于常规测井曲线的电相分析方法，以实现稳健且可复现的地下地层表征。

Method: 利用K-means聚类算法对Well C井的六条常规测井曲线（约11,195个采样点）进行多变量分析。通过惯性指标和轮廓系数对聚类结构进行评估，最终确定最佳聚类数目。

Result: 确定最佳聚类数为四个，平均轮廓系数约为0.50，表明聚类具有中等但显著的分离度。识别出的电相展现出与粘土含量、孔隙度及岩石骨架性质变化相关的系统性深度连续模式，构成了从页岩主导到清洁砂岩主导的地质连续体。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅依靠测井数据的无监督聚类方法结合定量评价指标，能够为缺乏岩心数据的区域提供一个稳健、可复现的地层表征框架，是勘探初期地层评价的实用工具。

Abstract: This study presents an unsupervised machine learning workflow for electrofacies analysis in the offshore Keta Basin, Ghana, where core data are scarce. Six standard wireline logs from Well~C were analysed over a depth interval comprising approximately $11{,}195$ samples. K-means clustering was applied in multivariate log space, with the clustering structure evaluated using inertia and silhouette diagnostics. Four clusters were identified, supported by an average silhouette coefficient of approximately $0.50$, indicating moderate but meaningful separation. The resulting electrofacies exhibit systematic, depth-continuous patterns associated with variations in clay content, porosity, and rock framework properties, forming a geological continuum from shale-dominated to cleaner sandstone-dominated units. The results demonstrate that log-only, unsupervised clustering supported by quantitative metrics provides a robust and reproducible framework for subsurface characterisation. The proposed workflow offers a practical tool for early-stage formation evaluation in frontier offshore basins and a foundation for future integrated studies.

</details>


### [121] [Language Models Refine Mechanical Linkage Designs Through Symbolic Reflection and Modular Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27962)
*João Pedro Gandarela,Thiago Rios,Stefan Menzel,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合语言模型和数值优化器的模块化架构，通过符号表示系统性地改进连杆机构设计，在六个运动目标上几何误差降低高达68%，结构有效性提升高达134%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械连杆机构设计涉及组合拓扑选择和连续参数拟合的复杂问题，传统方法难以有效处理离散拓扑探索和连续参数优化的结合。研究者希望利用语言模型的推理能力来系统性地改进连杆机构设计，探索AI在工程设计中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用模块化架构，语言模型智能体探索离散拓扑结构，数值优化器拟合连续参数。引入符号提升算子将模拟器轨迹转换为定性描述符、运动标签、时间谓词和结构诊断，使模型能够在迭代设计周期中解释和改进设计。使用三个开源模型（Llama 3.3 70B、Qwen3 4B、Qwen3 MoE 30B-A3B）进行验证。

Result: 在六个工程相关的运动目标上，模块化架构相比单体基线几何误差降低高达68%，结构有效性提升高达134%。78.6%的迭代优化轨迹显示出可测量的改进，系统正确诊断了过约束(56.3%)和欠约束(35.6%)失效模式并提出有依据的修正方案。所有三个模型家族都能在无微调情况下获得可解释的机械推理策略。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于原则的符号抽象能够有效桥接生成式AI与工程设计所需的数值精度，为AI驱动的工程设计提供了可行路径。语言模型无需微调即可获得可解释的机械推理策略，展现了AI在复杂工程设计问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Designing mechanical linkages involves combinatorial topology selection and continuous parameter fitting. We show that language models can systematically improve linkage designs through symbolic representations. Language model agents explore discrete topologies while numerical optimisers fit continuous parameters. A symbolic lifting operator translates simulator trajectories into qualitative descriptors, motion labels, temporal predicates, and structural diagnostics that models interpret across iterative design cycles. Across six engineering-relevant motion targets and three open-source models (Llama 3.3 70B, Qwen3 4B, Qwen3 MoE 30B-A3B), the modular architecture reduces geometric error by up to 68% and improves structural validity by up to 134% over monolithic baselines. Critically, 78.6% of iterative refinement trajectories show measurable improvement, with the system correctly diagnosing overconstraint (56.3%) and underconstraint (35.6%) failure modes and proposing grounded corrections. Models across all three families acquire interpretable mechanical reasoning strategies without fine-tuning, demonstrating that principled symbolic abstraction bridges generative AI and the numerical precision required for engineering design.

</details>


### [122] [TRUST: A Framework for Decentralized AI Service v.0.1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27132)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Zhen Tan,Mohan Zhang,Pingzhi Li,Zhuo Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST，一个去中心化的AI审计框架，通过分层DAG、DAAN协议和多层级共识机制，解决了中心化审计在鲁棒性、可扩展性、透明度和隐私方面的局限性，在准确性和根因归因上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高风险领域的大型推理模型(LRMs)和多智能体系统(MAS)，现有中心化验证方法存在四大局限：(1)鲁棒性不足，存在单点故障风险；(2)可扩展性差，推理复杂性造成瓶颈；(3)不透明，隐藏审计侵蚀信任；(4)隐私风险，暴露推理轨迹可能导致模型窃取。

Method: 提出TRUST框架，包含三项核心创新：(i)分层有向无环图(HDAGs)将链式思维推理分解为五个抽象层级以实现并行分布式审计；(ii)DAAN协议将多智能体交互映射为因果交互图(CIGs)以实现确定性的根因归因；(iii)计算检查器、LLM评估器和人类专家之间的多层共识机制，采用权益加权投票，可在30%对抗参与下保证正确性。证明了安全-盈利定理，确保诚实审计者获利而恶意行为者受损。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准测试中，TRUST达到72.4%的准确率（较基线高4-18%），并在20%腐败率下保持韧性。DAAN达到70%的根因归因率（标准方法为54-63%），且节省60%的token。人类研究验证了设计的有效性（F1=0.89，Brier=0.074）。

Conclusion: 该框架支持去中心化审计、防篡改排行榜、无信任数据注释和受治理的自主智能体，开创了去中心化AI审计的先河，为推理能力系统的安全、可问责部署奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) and Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) in high-stakes domains demand reliable verification, yet centralized approaches suffer four limitations: (1) Robustness, with single points of failure vulnerable to attacks and bias; (2) Scalability, as reasoning complexity creates bottlenecks; (3) Opacity, as hidden auditing erodes trust; and (4) Privacy, as exposed reasoning traces risk model theft. We introduce TRUST (Transparent, Robust, and Unified Services for Trustworthy AI), a decentralized framework with three innovations: (i) Hierarchical Directed Acyclic Graphs (HDAGs) that decompose Chain-of-Thought reasoning into five abstraction levels for parallel distributed auditing; (ii) the DAAN protocol, which projects multi-agent interactions into Causal Interaction Graphs (CIGs) for deterministic root-cause attribution; and (iii) a multi-tier consensus mechanism among computational checkers, LLM evaluators, and human experts with stake-weighted voting that guarantees correctness under 30% adversarial participation. We prove a Safety-Profitability Theorem ensuring honest auditors profit while malicious actors incur losses. All decisions are recorded on-chain, while privacy-by-design segmentation prevents reconstruction of proprietary logic. Across multiple LLMs and benchmarks, TRUST attains 72.4% accuracy (4-18% above baselines) and remains resilient against 20% corruption. DAAN reaches 70% root-cause attribution (vs. 54-63% for standard methods) with 60% token savings. Human studies validate the design (F1 = 0.89, Brier = 0.074). The framework supports (A1) decentralized auditing, (A2) tamper-proof leaderboards, (A3) trustless data annotation, and (A4) governed autonomous agents, pioneering decentralized AI auditing for safe, accountable deployment of reasoning-capable systems.

</details>


### [123] [Unpacking Vibe Coding: Help-Seeking Processes in Student-AI Interactions While Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27134)
*Daiana Rinja,Eduardo Araujo Oliveira,Sonsoles López-Pernas,Mohammed Saqr,Marcus Specht,Kamila Misiejuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析本科生与AI的编程交互序列，发现高表现学生采用工具性求助（探究式），而低表现学生依赖执行性求助（获取现成答案），表明当前生成式AI会镜像反映学生的意图而非优化学习，呼吁设计具有教学对齐性的AI系统以引导学生进行探究性交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI重塑高等教育编程教学，出现了“氛围编码”现象，学生通过自然语言与AI协作而非逐行编写代码。研究旨在探究这种新型人机交互模式下的求助行为差异及其对学习效果的影响，为AI教育工具的设计提供依据。

Method: 研究收集了110名本科生与AI交互的19,418个交互轮次，采用归纳编码方法对交互内容进行分类，并运用异质转移网络分析（Heterogeneous Transition Network Analysis）技术考察交互序列模式，对比高表现与低表现学生的交互策略差异。

Result: 高表现学生采用工具性求助策略，通过提问和探索引导AI扮演导师角色；低表现学生则依赖执行性求助，频繁将任务委托给AI，促使AI扮演执行者角色并提供现成解决方案。研究发现当前生成式AI倾向于镜像反映学生意图（无论是否具有建设性），而非主动优化学习过程。

Conclusion: 当前生成式AI系统作为被动顺从的工具，未能有效促进深度学习。研究主张AI系统应从工具演变为协作者，设计具有教学对齐性的系统，能够检测非生产性的任务委托，并自适应地将教育交互引导至探究方向，确保学生-AI伙伴关系增强而非取代学生的认知努力。

Abstract: Generative AI is reshaping higher education programming through vibe coding, where students collaborate with AI via natural language rather than writing code line-by-line. We conceptualize this practice as help-seeking, analyzing 19,418 interaction turns from 110 undergraduate students. Using inductive coding and Heterogeneous Transition Network Analysis, we examined interaction sequences to compare top- and low-performing students. Results reveal that top performers engaged in instrumental help-seeking -- inquiry and exploration -- eliciting tutor-like AI responses. In contrast, low performers relied on executive help-seeking, frequently delegating tasks and prompting the AI to assume an executor role focused on ready-made solutions. These findings indicate that currently generative AI mirrors student intent (whether productive or passive) rather than optimizing for learning. To evolve from tools to teammates, AI systems must move beyond passive compliance. We argue for pedagogically aligned design that detect unproductive delegation and adaptively steer educational interactions toward inquiry, ensuring student-AI partnerships augment rather than replace cognitive effort.

</details>


### [124] [Optimal Stop-Loss and Take-Profit Parameterization for Autonomous Trading Agent Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27150)
*Nathan Li,Aikins Laryea,Yigit Ihlamur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动加密货币交易系统中的退出策略优化问题，发现更严格的止损、更早的止盈以及更紧密的追踪保护能够显著提升风险调整后的收益，并提出了一个更规范的退出逻辑调优框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动加密货币交易系统往往将设计重心放在入场策略上，而退出策略（止损和止盈）则依赖很少经过系统性测试的固定规则。作者旨在探究优化退出设置是否能提升交易代理群体的性能。

Method: 研究收集了超过900笔历史交易记录，通过回放机制在多种替代退出策略下重新模拟每笔交易，并将结果与现有生产环境配置进行对比。在评估过程中，研究最初采用了按时间顺序划分数据的方法，但发现最新的交易数据处于由战争驱动的异常市场时期，导致结果失真。为了减少该单一事件的影响，作者改用随机数据划分进行主要对比分析，并明确承认了该方法的局限性。

Result: 研究发现退出策略的设计对交易性能有显著影响。表现更优的配置通常倾向于：设置更紧的止损限制、更早地锁定利润以及采用更紧密的追踪保护。这些配置能够改善风险调整后的业绩表现。

Conclusion: 退出策略的设计意义重大，不应被忽视。本文提出了一个实用框架，能够以更规范、透明的方式调整和优化交易系统的退出逻辑，同时也强调了在评估过程中需警惕特殊市场时期对回测结果的扭曲效应。

Abstract: Autonomous crypto trading systems often spend most of their design effort on finding entries, while exits are left to fixed rules that are rarely tested in a systematic way. This paper examines whether better stop-loss and take-profit settings can improve the performance of an autonomous trading agent swarm. Using more than 900 historical trades, we replay each trade under many alternative exit policies and compare results against the existing production setup. The study finds that exit design matters meaningfully: stronger configurations improve risk-adjusted performance and generally favor tighter loss limits, earlier profit capture, and closer trailing protection. The paper also discusses a key evaluation challenge: a purely chronological split was initially used, but the newest trades fell into an unusual war-driven market period that sharply distorted test results. To reduce the influence of that single episode, the main comparison was run on randomized data, with the drawbacks of doing so acknowledged explicitly. Overall, the paper presents a practical framework for tuning exit logic in a more disciplined and transparent way.

</details>


### [125] [Step-level Optimization for Efficient Computer-use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27151)
*Jinbiao Wei,Kangqi Ni,Yilun Zhao,Guo Gan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种事件驱动的级联框架，通过轻量级监视器（卡顿监视器和里程碑监视器）检测高风险时刻，默认运行小策略，仅在必要时调用大模型，从而高效地解决计算机使用代理计算成本高昂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用代理（Computer-use agents）在执行长周期GUI任务时，不加区分地在几乎每一步都调用大型多模态模型，导致计算成本高昂且速度缓慢。这种均匀的计算分配是低效的，因为任务轨迹具有高度异构性：许多步骤是常规的，可由小型廉价策略处理，错误仅集中在少数高风险时刻（表现为进度停滞和语义漂移）。

Method: 提出了一个事件驱动的步骤级级联框架。该框架默认运行小型策略，仅当轻量级学习监视器检测到风险升高时，才升级至更强的模型。框架结合了两个互补信号：1）卡顿监视器（Stuck Monitor），从最近的推理-行动历史中检测进度退化并触发恢复；2）里程碑监视器（Milestone Monitor），识别语义上有意义的检查点，以进行稀疏验证来捕捉语义漂移。该设计将“始终开启”的前沿模型推理转变为自适应的按需计算分配，且具有模块化和部署导向特性，可直接叠加在现有代理之上。

Result: 该框架能够将原本昂贵的“始终开启”的前沿模型推理转化为自适应的按需计算分配，有效解决了长周期GUI任务中计算资源分配的低效问题，并能捕捉进度停滞和语义漂移两种主要失败模式。

Conclusion: 该框架是模块化且面向部署的，无需更改底层代理架构或重新训练大模型，即可直接应用于现有的计算机使用代理，实现了更高效、经济的计算资源利用。

Abstract: Computer-use agents provide a promising path toward general software automation because they can interact directly with arbitrary graphical user interfaces instead of relying on brittle, application-specific integrations. Despite recent advances in benchmark performance, strong computer-use agents remain expensive and slow in practice, since most systems invoke large multimodal models at nearly every interaction step. We argue that this uniform allocation of compute is fundamentally inefficient for long-horizon GUI tasks. Such trajectories are highly heterogeneous: many steps are routine and can be handled reliably by smaller, cheaper policies, while errors tend to concentrate at a relatively small number of high-risk moments. Across computer-use benchmarks, these failures repeatedly take two forms: progress stalls, where the agent loops, repeats ineffective actions, or fails to make meaningful progress, and silent semantic drift, where the agent continues taking locally plausible actions after already deviating from the user's true goal. To address this inefficiency, we propose an event-driven, step-level cascade for computer-use agents that runs a small policy by default and escalates to a stronger model only when lightweight learned monitors detect elevated risk. Our framework combines two complementary signals: a Stuck Monitor that detects degraded progress from recent reasoning-action history and triggers recovery, and a Milestone Monitor that identifies semantically meaningful checkpoints where sparse verification is most informative for catching drift. This design turns always-on frontier-model inference into adaptive, on-demand compute allocation over the course of an evolving interaction. The framework is modular and deployment-oriented: it can be layered on top of existing computer-use agents without changing the underlying agent architecture or retraining the large model.

</details>


### [126] [Interval Orders, Biorders and Credibility-limited Belief Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27156)
*Richard Booth,Ivan Varzinczak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将理性选择理论中的区间序和双序引入信念修正领域，提出了基于这两类更一般序关系的修正算子族并给出了公理刻画，通过将导致不一致的输入视为“不可信”，导出了满足一致性但不满足成功性公理的非优先修正新族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理性信念修正通常基于可能世界上的全预序，但在理性选择理论中存在更一般的序类（如区间序和双序），这些序类在信念修正中鲜有应用。探索这些更广泛序类的应用潜力，可丰富信念修正的理论基础并捕获传统模型难以表达的现象（如认知失调或不稳定性）。

Method: 作者首先引入Fishburn提出的区间序（为每个可能世界赋予非负“合理性区间”），继而引入Aleskerov等人的双序（允许区间具有负长度，用以刻画认知失调或不稳定性）。通过对这两类序及相关中间类进行公理刻画，定义相应的信念修正算子，并进一步通过修改定义（将导致不一致的输入标记为“不可信”）导出非优先修正算子族。

Result: 给出了基于区间序和双序的两族信念修正算子的公理刻画，以及介于二者之间的另外两族算子。证明了基于双序的修正满足成功性公理但不总能保持一致性；通过筛选不可信输入，获得了满足一致性但不满足成功性公理的非优先修正新族，其可信句子集不满足单句闭合条件。

Conclusion: 基于双序的方法适用于代理最初可能拒绝新信息、但在获得额外解释后可能接受该信息的场景。该框架与非优先修正和可信度受限修正相关，但拓展了可信句子集的结构限制，为信念修正提供了更灵活的工具。

Abstract: Rational belief revision is commonly viewed as being based on a preference order between possible worlds, with the resulting new belief set being those sentences true in all the most preferred models of the incoming new information. Usually, such a preference order is taken to be a total preorder. Nevertheless, there are other, more general classes of ordering that can also be employed. In this paper, we explore two such classes that have been studied within the theory of rational choice but have seen limited or no application in belief revision. We begin with interval orders, introduced by Fishburn in the '80s, which associate with each possible world a nonnegative `interval' of plausibility. We then move on to biorders, studied by Aleskerov, Bouyssou, and Monjardet, which generalise interval orders by allowing the intervals to have negative lengths, a feature that can be used to capture a notion of dissonance or instability. We provide axiomatic characterisations of these two resulting families of belief revision operators, as well as of two further families of interest that lie between interval orders and biorders. We show that while biorder-based revisions satisfy the Success postulate, they do not always yield consistent outputs. By modifying their definition to discard inputs that lead to inconsistency as `incredible', we derive new families of so-called non-prioritised revision that satisfy the Consistency postulate, but not the Success one. These families are linked to credibility-limited revision operators of Hansson et al., but for which the set of credible sentences does not satisfy the single-sentence closure condition. We argue that the biorder-based approach is well-suited for scenarios where an agent might initially reject new information, but may accept it when presented with additional explanation.

</details>


### [127] [Evaluating TabPFN for Mild Cognitive Impairment to Alzheimer's Disease Conversion in Data Limited Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27195)
*Brad Ye,Bulent Soykan,Gulsah Hancerliogullari Koksalmis,Hsin-Hsiung Huang,Laura J. Brattain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了TabPFN在MCI到阿尔茨海默病（AD）转化预测任务中的表现，发现其在有限纵向数据场景下优于传统机器学习模型，尤其是在小样本训练集中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测轻度认知障碍（MCI）向阿尔茨海默病（AD）的转化对于早期干预至关重要，但由于纵向数据的有限性，开发可靠的转化预测模型面临挑战。

Method: 使用来自ADNI的TADPOLE数据集，提取人口统计学、APOE4、MRI体积、CSF标志物和PET成像的多模态生物标志物特征。在训练集大小（N=50至1000）和模型（包括XGBoost、随机森林、LightGBM和逻辑回归）上对TabPFN（表格预训练基础网络）与传统机器学习方法进行了实验比较。

Result: TabPFN实现了最高的性能（AUC=0.892），优于LightGBM（AUC=0.860），并在低数据环境下表现出优势。在N=50的小训练样本下，TabPFN保持了较强的AUC，而传统机器学习模型在小样本训练中表现不佳。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基础模型在数据有限的疾病预测场景（如阿尔茨海默病）中具有广阔的应用前景。
