Updated LLM-generated reward-candidate experiments show candidate-family-dependent behavior: generated pools can exhibit phase-dependent winner changes, but no fixed warm-up schedule is universally optimal. Held-out schedule selection, conservative selector baselines, compute-matched controls, and scale controls further show that \textsc{RHyVE} is best understood as a verification-informed deployment protocol rather than a universal scheduler. Dense and all-failure boundary experiments delimit the scope of the method. Together, these results suggest that reward generation and reward deployment should be studied as coupled problems: generated rewards must be verified and deployed under changing policy competence.

</details>


### [197] [Characterizing the Consistency of the Emergent Misalignment Persona](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28082)
*Anietta Weckauff,Yuchen Zhang,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了新兴不对齐现象中模型行为与自我认知的一致性，发现了两种模式：行为与认知一致的“连贯人格模型”和行为有害但自认为对齐的“倒置人格模型”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现新兴不对齐模型中存在有害行为与自我评估的相关性，但这种对应关系在不同任务和微调领域的一致性尚不明确。需要系统性地表征EM人格的一致性。

Method: 在六个狭义不对齐领域（如不安全代码、危险金融建议、糟糕医疗建议等）上微调Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct模型，并进行有害性评估、自我评估、AI系统描述选择、输出识别和分数预测等实验。

Result: 发现了两种截然不同的模式：连贯人格模型中，有害行为与自我报告的不对齐相互耦合；倒置人格模型则产生有害输出，同时自我认定为对齐的AI系统。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了新兴不对齐效应更细致的画面，对EM人格的一致性提出了质疑，表明模型的有害行为与自我认知之间的关系比先前认知的更为复杂。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly misaligned data generalizes to broadly misaligned behavior, a phenomenon termed emergent misalignment (EM). While prior work has found a correlation between harmful behavior and self-assessment in emergently misaligned models, it remains unclear how consistent this correspondence is across tasks and whether it varies across fine-tuning domains. We characterize the consistency of the EM persona by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned domains (e.g., insecure code, risky financial advice, bad medical advice) and administering experiments including harmfulness evaluation, self-assessment, choosing between two descriptions of AI systems, output recognition, and score prediction. Our results reveal two distinct patterns: coherent-persona models, in which harmful behavior and self-reported misalignment are coupled, and inverted-persona models, which produce harmful outputs while identifying as aligned AI systems. These findings reveal a more fine-grained picture of the effects of emergent misalignment, calling into question the consistency of the EM persona.

</details>


### [198] [What Makes a Good Terminal-Agent Benchmark Task: A Guideline for Adversarial, Difficult, and Legible Evaluation Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28093)
*Ivan Bercovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对终端智能体基准测试任务编写，指出将任务编写视为提示词编写是错误的，提出了优质任务应具备对抗性、难度和清晰性，并详细列举了常见失败模式，发现超过15%的任务存在奖励可破解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着评估环境市场增长，基准任务发布压力大且缺乏充分的对抗性审查，导致大量低质量任务出现；作者基于一年多的贡献和审阅经验，发现大多数人错误地将基准任务编写等同于提示词编写，亟需提出正确指导原则。

Method: 系统性地编目常见失败模式（包括AI生成指令、过度规定性规范、文书困难、依赖隐藏知识的预言机解决方案、验证错误事物的测试、以及奖励可破解环境），并论证真实难度应源于概念层面而非环境层面，同时引用实证数据支持观点。

Result: 识别并分类了六大类常见失败模式；实证证据表明，在流行的终端智能体基准测试中，超过15%的任务存在奖励可破解问题；论证了优质基准任务应具备对抗性、难度和清晰性三个核心特性。

Conclusion: 基准任务编写与提示词编写有本质区别：提示词旨在帮助智能体成功，而基准任务旨在检验其能力；本文为基准维护者、任务贡献者及使用基准分数的研究人员提供了实用参考指南。

Abstract: Terminal-agent benchmarks have become a primary signal for measuring the coding and system-administration capabilities of large language models. As the market for evaluation environments grows, so does the pressure to ship tasks quickly, often without thorough adversarial review of the verification logic. This paper is a guideline for writing good benchmark tasks, drawn from over a year of contributing to and reviewing tasks for Terminal Bench. Most people write benchmark tasks the way they write prompts. They shouldn't. A prompt is designed to help the agent succeed; a benchmark is designed to find out if it can. We argue that good tasks are adversarial, difficult, and legible, and that a large class of common failure modes -- AI-generated instructions, over-prescriptive specifications, clerical difficulty, oracle solutions that assume hidden knowledge, tests that validate the wrong things, and reward-hackable environments -- are predictable consequences of treating task authoring as prompt authoring. We catalog these failure modes, argue that real difficulty is conceptual rather than environmental, and discuss recent empirical evidence that over 15% of tasks in popular terminal-agent benchmarks are reward-hackable. We hope this serves as a useful reference for benchmark maintainers, task contributors, and researchers using benchmark scores as evidence.

</details>


### [199] [Mapping the Methodological Space of Classroom Interaction Research: Scale, Duration, and Modality in an Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28098)
*Dorottya Demszky,Edith Bouton,Alison Twiner,Sara Hennessy,Richard Correnti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个三维框架（规模、持续时间、模态）来映射课堂互动研究的方法论空间，并用AI技术拓展了研究范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂互动研究长期分为大规模观察和深度民族志两种取向，缺乏统一的方法论框架来理解不同研究方法的优劣与适用边界。

Method: 构建了一个包含规模、持续时间、模态三个维度的方法论空间框架；通过对对话教学的两个对比研究案例（Howe et al. 2019 与 Snell & Lefstein 2018）及其主要研究者的访谈进行验证；围绕可操作化、机制可见性、实践转化三个核心问题展开分析。

Result: 研究发现，研究在方法论空间中的位置决定了其能揭示和遮蔽的内容；AI技术正在扩展这一方法论空间的边界；该框架可以指导研究设计和工具开发。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究者理解和选择不同方法论路径提供了系统性视角，有助于更清晰地认识不同研究方法的价值与局限，同时为AI辅助的教育研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Research on classroom interaction has long been divided between large-scale observation and in-depth ethnographic work. We propose a framework mapping this methodological space along three dimensions--scale, duration, and modality--where a study's position shapes what it reveals and obscures. We illustrate it through contrasting studies of dialogic teaching--Howe et al. (2019) and Snell and Lefstein (2018)--and an interview with the lead researchers, organized around three questions: what can be operationalized, what mechanisms become visible, and what translates to practice. We then examine how AI is expanding this space and how the framework can guide research and tool design.

</details>


### [200] [Splitting Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks and Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28112)
*Matti Berthold,Lydia Blümel,Giovanni Buraglio,Anna Rapberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对引入支持关系的双极集合论证框架（BSAFs），提出了多种新颖的分裂技术，涵盖对集体攻击、集体支持以及两者兼有的分裂，并证明了其在主流论证语义下的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSAFs通过结合集体攻击与支持关系，显著提升了论证框架的表达能力，并能自然地刻画非平坦的基于假设的论证。这种表达能力的增强迫切需要开发新的分裂技术，以实现复杂论证问题的高效模块化求解。

Method: 作者以BSAFs为理论基础，研究并构建了三种分裂模式：针对集体攻击的分裂（推广了现有SETAFs分裂技术）、针对集体支持的分裂，以及对集体攻击和支持的同时分裂。研究为这些分裂设计了适宜的模式，并针对常见的论证语义进行了正确性证明。

Result: 成功建立了适用于BSAFs的多种分裂模式，并从理论上证明了这些分裂技术在主流论证语义下的正确性，为该类框架的模块化计算提供了可靠的理论支撑。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅将现有的SETAF分裂技术成功推广至更具表达力的BSAFs，还通过引入针对支持关系的分裂方法，完善了论证框架的分裂理论体系，为处理复杂论证系统提供了有效的分析工具。

Abstract: This work proposes novel splitting techniques for argumentation formalisms that incorporate supports between defeasible elements. We base our studies on bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs) which generalize argumentation frameworks with collective attacks (SETAFs), as well as bipolar argumentation frameworks (BAFs), by incorporating both collective attacks and supports. Notably, BSAFs establish a crucial link to structured argumentation as they naturally capture general (potentially non-flat) assumption-based argumentation. The increase in expressiveness calls for diverse forms of splitting. We consider splits over collective attacks (thereby generalizing the recently proposed splitting techniques for SETAFs), splits over collective supports, as well as splits over both collective attacks and supports. We establish suitable splitting schemata and prove their correctness for the most common argumentation semantics.

</details>


### [201] [Normativity and Productivism: Ableist Intelligence? A Degrowth Analysis of AI Sign Language Translation Tools for Deaf People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28125)
*Nina Seron-Abouelfadil,Poppy Fynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判性地分析了当前手语识别AI系统存在的问题，指出这些系统基于有偏见的数据构建且缺乏聋人群体的参与，实际上是一种"能力歧视智能"，通过技术理性化消灭了聋人的人际沟通体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手语识别和翻译AI系统虽然旨在提供无障碍沟通，但实际上基于偏见数据构建，缺乏聋人社区参与，忽视了手势语言中固有的文化、语义和口语化特征，听障使用者对这些系统的缺陷认识不足。

Method: 采用埃吕尔的技术系统和技术欺诈理论作为分析框架，考察技术如何将手语标准化为可被数据、统计和数学语言捕获的形式，以及这种理性化过程背后的利润驱动逻辑。

Result: 研究发现AI技术通过标准化和大众化过程产生了规范性效应，创造了一个必须适应工具和技术环境而非相反的"人"的模型。这种技术理性化消灭了沟通的条件，未能捕捉聋人的人类体验，表现出明显的反生产力特征——疏远而非解放，隔离而非滋养人际关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出AI应被理解为"能力歧视智能"的概念，因为这类系统试图强化手语的屈辱和边缘化性质，而非真正服务于聋人群体的沟通需求。技术的目标应该是让人适应技术，而非让人服务于技术的效率和生产力目标。

Abstract: Sign languages, of any geographical or accentual variation, understandably face continuous scrutiny under the ever present popularity of verbal dictation and audism. Through this, many potential problems arise with the current lack of accessible communication for those who rely on such sign languages for essential conversation. Such AI systems regularly take the form of recognition and interpretation models, designed to provide seamless and accurate translation. In reality these systems are built from biased data and created without any input from deaf communities. Such models are widely used and accepted by their hearing counterparts who remain ignorant to the inherent culture, semantics and colloquial language present in gestural language systems.
  This phenomenon is best analysed under the scope of The Technological System and Technological bluff by Ellul. Indeed, what is at play here is the standardization of language by technicians into what can be captured by technique: data, statistics, a mathematical language. For that AI technique to exist, sign language must be rationalized, in a search for profit that annihilates the conditions for communication and fails to capture the human experience of the deaf person. By that process, it presents normative effects, creating a model of Man, standardized, massified, and who has to adapt to the tool and technical milieu instead of the other way around, which we assume should have been the goal of such a technology.
  Technique thus reshapes what it means to be human, to submit deaf people to the goals of productivity and efficiency. In doing so, it exhibits clear counter productivity, alienating instead of emancipating, isolating instead of nourishing human relationships. Therefore this paper argues for the idea of AI as Ableist Intelligence, as such systems seek to emphasise the humiliated and marginalised nature of sign.

</details>


### [202] [Intern-Atlas: A Methodological Evolution Graph as Research Infrastructure for AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28158)
*Yujun Wu,Dongxu Zhang,Xinchen Li,Jinhang Xu,Yiling Duan,Yumou Liu,Jiabao Pan,Xuanhe Zhou,Jingxuan Wei,Siyuan Li,Jintao Chen,Conghui He,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Intern-Atlas，一个方法论演化图谱，通过自动识别方法实体、推断谱系关系并捕捉驱动方法创新过渡的瓶颈，构建了一个包含超过940万条语义类型边的可查询因果网络，为AI驱动的科学发现提供基础数据层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究基础设施以文档为中心，仅提供论文间的引用链接，缺乏对方法论演化的显式表示，无法捕捉方法如何以及为何产生、适应和构建彼此的结构化关系。随着AI驱动的研究代理成为科学知识的新消费者，这一局限性日益凸显，因为此类代理无法可靠地从非结构化文本中重建方法演化拓扑。

Method: 构建Intern-Atlas方法论演化图谱：(1)从103万篇AI论文中自动识别方法级实体；(2)推断方法论之间的谱系关系；(3)捕捉驱动连续创新之间过渡的瓶颈；(4)生成基于原文证据的语义类型边。此外，提出自引导时序树搜索算法构建追溯方法时间进展的演化链。

Result: 构建了包含9,410,201条语义类型边的方法论演化图谱，每条边均基于原文证据。与专家策划的真实演化链对比评估显示高度一致性。验证了Intern-Atlas在想法评估和自动化想法生成等下游应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 方法论演化图谱可作为新兴自动化科学发现的基础数据层，为AI驱动的研究代理提供结构化的方法演化知识，使其能够可靠地理解和追溯方法的发展脉络，从而推动科学发现的自动化进程。

Abstract: Existing research infrastructure is fundamentally document-centric, providing citation links between papers but lacking explicit representations of methodological evolution. In particular, it does not capture the structured relationships that explain how and why research methods emerge, adapt, and build upon one another. With the rise of AI-driven research agents as a new class of consumers of scientific knowledge, this limitation becomes increasingly consequential, as such agents cannot reliably reconstruct method evolution topologies from unstructured text. We introduce Intern-Atlas, a methodological evolution graph that automatically identifies method-level entities, infers lineage relationships among methodologies, and captures the bottlenecks that drive transitions between successive innovations. Built from 1,030,314 papers spanning AI conferences, journals, and arXiv preprints, the resulting graph comprises 9,410,201 semantically typed edges, each grounded in verbatim source evidence, forming a queryable causal network of methodological development. To operationalize this structure, we further propose a self-guided temporal tree search algorithm for constructing evolution chains that trace the progression of methods over time. We evaluate the quality of the resulting graph against expert-curated ground-truth evolution chains and observe strong alignment. In addition, we demonstrate that Intern-Atlas enables downstream applications in idea evaluation and automated idea generation. We position methodological evolution graphs as a foundational data layer for the emerging automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [203] [LLM as Clinical Graph Structure Refiner: Enhancing Representation Learning in EEG Seizure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28178)
*Lincan Li,Zheng Chen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）作为图边缘优化器的两阶段框架，用于优化脑电图（EEG）信号的图结构构建，从而提升癫痫检测的准确性和图结构的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图（EEG）信号固有的噪声使得鲁棒的表示学习具有挑战性。现有的图构建方法（基于相关性或学习方法）由于EEG数据的噪声特性，常生成冗余或不相关的边缘，这严重影响了图表示的质量，并限制了下游任务的性能。受大语言模型（LLM）在推理和上下文理解方面显著能力的启发，研究者希望利用LLM来解决图边缘冗余问题。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：第一阶段，使用基于Transformer的边缘预测器和多层感知机（MLP）构建初始图，为潜在边缘分配概率分数并通过阈值确定其存在性；第二阶段，将LLM作为边缘集优化器，基于节点对的文本和统计特征，对初始图中的连接进行验证并去除冗余连接。

Result: 在TUSZ数据集上的大量实验表明，该LLM优化的图学习框架不仅显著提高了癫痫检测任务的准确率，还生成了更干净、更具可解释性的图结构。同时，验证了基于LLM的边缘优化能有效识别并移除冗余连接。

Conclusion: 本文成功验证了将大语言模型用于图边缘优化的可行性，证明LLM能有效提升含噪生物医学信号（如EEG）的图表示质量。所提框架在提升任务性能的同时增强了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) signals are vital for automated seizure detection, but their inherent noise makes robust representation learning challenging. Existing graph construction methods, whether correlation-based or learning-based, often generate redundant or irrelevant edges due to the noisy nature of EEG data. This significantly impairs the quality of graph representation and limits downstream task performance. Motivated by the remarkable reasoning and contextual understanding capabilities of large language models (LLMs), we explore the idea of using LLMs as graph edge refiners. Specifically, we propose a two-stage framework: we first verify that LLM-based edge refinement can effectively identify and remove redundant connections, leading to significant improvements in seizure detection accuracy and more meaningful graph structures. Building on this insight, we further develop a robust solution where the initial graph is constructed using a Transformer-based edge predictor and multilayer perceptron, assigning probability scores to potential edges and applying a threshold to determine their existence. The LLM then acts as an edge set refiner, making informed decisions based on both textual and statistical features of node pairs to validate the remaining connections. Extensive experiments on TUSZ dataset demonstrate that our LLM-refined graph learning framework not only enhances task performance but also yields cleaner and more interpretable graph representations.

</details>


### [204] [Synthetic Computers at Scale for Long-Horizon Productivity Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28181)
*Tao Ge,Baolin Peng,Hao Cheng,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“规模化的合成计算机”方法，通过构建逼真的用户计算机环境（包含文件夹层次结构和文档等工件）并运行长周期模拟（平均超过2000轮，耗时8小时以上），生成了丰富的智能体经验数据，显著提升了智能体在生产力任务中的表现，为智能体自我改进提供了可扩展的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的长周期生产力工作高度依赖于用户特定的计算机环境（如目录结构、文档工件），现有的合成数据创建方法难以覆盖这种复杂且个性化的场景。为了解决这一局限，需要一种可扩展的方法来生成逼真的用户环境及相应的长周期任务，以支持智能体的训练与自我改进。

Method: 提出了“规模化的合成计算机”方法论。首先，自动化构建包含逼真文件夹层次结构和内容丰富工件（文档、表格、演示文稿等）的合成计算机环境。然后，在该环境中运行长周期模拟：一个智能体负责生成针对该用户的、需要约一个月人力投入的生产力目标；另一个智能体扮演用户，在合成计算机中导航文件系统、协调模拟协作者并产出专业工件，直至完成任务。

Result: 在初步实验中，成功创建了1,000个合成计算机并运行了长周期模拟，每次模拟平均耗时超过8小时、跨度超过2,000轮。这些模拟产生了丰富的经验学习信号，使得智能体在领域内和领域外的生产力评估任务中均取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过规模化创建合成计算机并运行大规模模拟来生成长周期生产力数据的可行性与有效性。鉴于人物设定数据规模庞大，该方法理论上可扩展至数百万甚至数十亿个合成用户世界，为长周期生产力场景下的智能体自我改进和强化学习提供了极具潜力的基础底座。

Abstract: Realistic long-horizon productivity work is strongly conditioned on user-specific computer environments, where much of the work context is stored and organized through directory structures and content-rich artifacts. To scale synthetic data creation for such productivity scenarios, we introduce Synthetic Computers at Scale, a scalable methodology for creating such environments with realistic folder hierarchies and content-rich artifacts (e.g., documents, spreadsheets, and presentations). Conditioned on each synthetic computer, we run long-horizon simulations: one agent creates productivity objectives that are specific to the computer's user and require multiple professional deliverables and about a month of human work; another agent then acts as that user and keeps working across the computer -- for example, navigating the filesystem for grounding, coordinating with simulated collaborators, and producing professional artifacts -- until these objectives are completed.
  In preliminary experiments, we create 1,000 synthetic computers and run long-horizon simulations on them; each run requires over 8 hours of agent runtime and spans more than 2,000 turns on average. These simulations produce rich experiential learning signals, whose effectiveness is validated by significant improvements in agent performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain productivity evaluations. Given that personas are abundant at billion scale, this methodology can in principle scale to millions or even billions of synthetic user worlds with sufficient compute, enabling broader coverage of diverse professions, roles, contexts, environments, and productivity needs. We argue that scalable synthetic computer creation, together with at-scale simulations, is highly promising as a foundational substrate for agent self-improvement and agentic reinforcement learning in long-horizon productivity scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Efficient Training on Multiple Consumer GPUs with RoundPipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27085)
*Yibin Luo,Shiwei Gao,Huichuan Zheng,Youyou Lu,Jiwu Shu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RoundPipe，一种面向消费级GPU的新型流水线调度方案，通过打破权重绑定约束实现近零气泡流水线，在8卡RTX 4090上相比基线实现1.48-2.16倍加速，并首次在单服务器上支持Qwen3-235B模型的LoRA微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级GPU上微调大语言模型（LLMs）具有成本效益优势，但受限于显存容量不足和PCIe互连带宽较低。现有的流水线并行（PP）结合CPU卸载方案存在“权重绑定问题”——将负载不均的模型阶段（如大型语言模型头部）绑定到特定GPU，导致流水线吞吐量受限于最重负载的GPU，产生严重的流水线气泡。

Method: 提出RoundPipe调度方案，核心创新包括：(1) 将GPU视为无状态执行工作池，以轮询方式动态分发计算阶段，打破权重绑定约束；(2) 设计优先级感知的数据传输调度引擎；(3) 提出细粒度分布式事件同步协议确保训练正确性；(4) 开发自动化层划分算法优化系统效率。该方法实现了近零气泡的流水线并行。

Result: 在8×RTX 4090服务器上的评估表明：(1) 对1.7B至32B模型微调，相比最先进基线实现1.48-2.16倍加速；(2) 成功在单服务器上实现Qwen3-235B模型（序列长度31K）的LoRA微调，这是此前无法实现的规模。RoundPipe已作为开源Python库发布，附带完整文档。

Conclusion: RoundPipe有效解决了消费级GPU服务器上大模型微调的流水线气泡问题，通过创新的调度策略显著提升了训练效率，使得在有限的硬件资源上微调超大模型成为可能，为大模型民主化应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on consumer-grade GPUs is highly cost-effective, yet constrained by limited GPU memory and slow PCIe interconnects. Pipeline parallelism combined with CPU offloading mitigates these hardware bottlenecks by reducing communication overhead. However, existing PP schedules suffer from an inherent limitation termed the weight binding issue. Binding uneven model stages (e.g., the LM head is large) to GPUs limits the pipeline's throughput to that of the GPU with the heaviest load, leading to severe pipeline bubbles.
  In this paper, we propose RoundPipe, a novel pipeline schedule that breaks the weight binding constraint on consumer GPU servers. RoundPipe treats GPUs as a pool of stateless execution workers and dynamically dispatches computation stages across devices in a round-robin manner, achieving a near-zero-bubble pipeline. To ensure training correctness and system efficiency, RoundPipe integrates a priority-aware transfer scheduling engine, a fine-grained distributed event-based synchronization protocol, and an automated layer partitioning algorithm. Evaluations on an 8$\times$ RTX 4090 server demonstrate that RoundPipe achieves 1.48--2.16$\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art baselines when fine-tuning 1.7B to 32B models. Remarkably, RoundPipe enables LoRA fine-tuning of the Qwen3-235B model with 31K sequence length on a single server.
  RoundPipe is publicly available as an open-source Python library with comprehensive documentation.

</details>


### [206] [End-to-End and Phase-Level Performance Optimization for Hyperledger Fabric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27174)
*Pavan Sollu,Aniruddha Mukherjee,Divya Pulivarthi,S. R. Eshwar,Gugan Thoppe,Kshitij Pratihast,Tittu Varghese,Hrishikesh Nashikkar,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对Hyperledger Fabric进行了系统性的全流程优化研究，提出了两种针对提交阶段的优化技术（块级流水线和策略等待），吞吐量分别提升1.9倍和1.2倍，并通过实验揭示了私有数据传播、区块大小选择和背书节点选择等配置参数的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperledger Fabric作为企业级许可链被广泛采用，但其吞吐量和延迟优化仍面临挑战，主要困难在于交易生命周期中某一阶段的优化决策可能对其他阶段产生负面影响，缺乏系统性的优化指导。

Method: 采用生产级测试床实验与校准的SimPy仿真相结合的方法，从三个维度进行研究：1）提出块级流水线和策略等待两种提交阶段优化技术；2）对私有数据传播、区块大小选择、背书节点选择三个配置参数进行微基准测试；3）分析私有数据传播、VSCC并行化与流水线提交之间的相互作用。

Result: 1）块级流水线通过重叠验证和数据获取与状态一致性检查，将提交吞吐量提升最高1.9倍；策略等待通过协调提交进度提升吞吐量1.2倍；2）私有数据传播的宽松仲裁显著降低背书和提交延迟；轻负载下小区块延迟更低，重负载下大区块提升吞吐量；宽松的主节点选择显著减少交易丢弃并提升背书吞吐量；3）流水线提交相比串行提交的吞吐量增益在中等并行化水平时达到最大化。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了面向阶段和协议层的HLF优化方案，揭示了配置参数间的复杂交互关系，为企业级区块链部署提供了实用的优化指导原则。

Abstract: Hyperledger Fabric (HLF) is a modular, permissioned blockchain widely adopted in enterprise settings. Enhancing its throughput and latency remains challenging, as optimization decisions made in one phase of the transaction lifecycle can adversely affect other phases. In this work, we present a systematic, phase-level and end-to-end study of HLF optimizations along three fronts, combining production-grade testbed experiments with calibrated SimPy simulations. First, we introduce two novel optimization techniques that target commit-phase bottlenecks: block-level pipelining and strategic waiting. In pipelining, we overlap validation and private-data acquisition of successive blocks with state-consistency checks and ledger updates improving commit throughput by up to 1.9x. Strategic waiting coordinates commit progress by temporarily pausing fast leaders and boosting laggers to sustain endorsement parallelism, yielding up to a 1.2x higher throughput. Second, we conduct micro-benchmarking of three configuration levers: private-data dissemination, block-size selection, and endorsement peer selection. Our results reveal that: (i) Relaxed quorums for private-data dissemination significantly reduce latency in both endorsement and commit phases; (ii) Under light workloads, smaller blocks yield lower end-to-end latency, whereas, under heavy workloads, larger blocks are necessary to improve throughput and reduce latency; and (iii) Relaxed leader selection dramatically reduces dropped transactions and boosts endorsement throughput, with a modest increase in MVCC invalidations. Finally, we analyze the interplay among private-data dissemination, VSCC parallelization, and pipelined commits. Interestingly, the throughput gains over a serial commit path are maximized at a moderate level of parallelization. Together, our findings provide phase-aware and protocol-level refinements for optimizing HLF.

</details>


### [207] [Towards the Democratization and Standardization of Dynamic Resources with MPI Spawning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27430)
*Sergio Iserte,Iker Martín-Alvarez,Krzystof Rojek,José I. Aliaga,Maribel Castillo,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于高性能计算（HPC）动态资源管理的统一API工具，通过集成Proteo重配置引擎增强了DMR框架的灵活性，支持多种重配置策略且无需重启所有进程，并在MPDATA应用上验证了其性能和编程效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态资源管理方案存在灵活性不足、对不同资源管理器支持有限、以及重配置时需要重启所有进程或缺乏RMS支持等局限性，亟需一种更加灵活、适应性强且用户友好的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个统一的动态资源管理API，在原有DMRlib结构基础上演进DMR框架，使其支持多种动态资源管理器，并集成Proteo重配置引擎以增强可塑性策略，实现无需重生所有进程的多样化重配置方法。

Result: 成功实现了支持不同重配置管理器的模块化DMR框架，升级后的DMRlib配合Proteo引擎提供了广泛的重配置策略，并开发了可塑性版本的MPDATA求解器；通过MPDATA应用展示了该解决方案的性能优势及编码生产力的提升。

Conclusion: 该统一API和增强的DMR框架有效解决了以往动态资源管理的局限性，实现了灵活、高效且易于集成的动态资源管理，为HPC应用提供了更好的可塑性和用户体验。

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient tool for managing dynamic resources in production high-performance computing (HPC) settings, focusing on flexibility, adaptability, and user-friendliness. We introduce a unified dynamic resource management application programming interface (API) that supports a wide range of HPC applications, allowing seamless integration without direct interaction with Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR). The DMR framework, evolved from the DMRlib structure, now supports various dynamic resource managers and includes the Proteo reconfiguration engine to enhance malleability strategies. This integration addresses previous limitations by allowing diverse reconfiguration methods without respawning all processes or lacking RMS support. The paper also showcases the solution's performance and coding productivity with the MPDATA (Multidimensional Positive Definite Advection Transport Algorithm) application. Key contributions include an enhanced modular DMR framework supporting different reconfiguration managers, upgraded DMRlib with the Proteo reconfiguration engine, offering extensive reconfiguration strategies, and a malleable version of the MPDATA solver.

</details>


### [208] [A Study on the Performance of Distributed Training of Data-driven CFD Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27431)
*Sergio Iserte,Alejandro González-Barberá,Paloma Barreda,Krzysztof Rojek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对比了CPU、多GPU和分布式三种训练方式在流体模拟时间序列预测深度学习模型上的性能，证明分布式GPU训练能显著加速模型训练，实现高精度流体状态的快速预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于偏微分方程（PDE）求解的物理行为模拟方法计算量大、耗时长。尽管数据驱动方法利用AI技术能加速预测，但其训练阶段的成本不容忽视。因此，有必要研究如何优化训练过程，降低数据驱动方法的训练开销。

Method: 以特定流体模拟为用例，构建时间序列预测深度学习模型来推断流体未来状态。通过代码适配，系统性地比较了仅CPU、多GPU和分布式三种训练方案的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，分布式GPU训练方案在不同实现中均表现出明显优势，能够在保证预测高精度状态的同时，将预测时间缩短至仅占传统计算流体动力学（CFD）求解器所需时间的一小部分。

Conclusion: 分布式GPU训练是加速数据驱动流体模拟的有效途径，通过合理的代码适配，可以大幅降低训练成本，同时保持高精度的预测能力，为科学计算领域的数据驱动方法应用提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Data-driven methods for computer simulations are blooming in many scientific areas. The traditional approach to simulating physical behaviors relies on solving partial differential equations (PDE). Since calculating these iterative equations is highly both computationally demanding and time-consuming, data-driven methods leverage artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to alleviate that workload. Data-driven methods have to be trained in advance to provide their subsequent fast predictions, however, the cost of the training stage is non-negligible. This paper presents a predictive model for inferencing future states of a specific fluid simulation that serves as a use case for evaluating different training alternatives. Particularly, this study compares the performance of only CPU, multiGPU, and distributed approaches for training a time series forecasting deep learning (DL) model. With some slight code adaptations, results show and compare, in different implementations, the benefits of distributed GPU-enabled training for predicting high-accuracy states in a fraction of the time needed by the computational fluid dynamics (CFD) solver.

</details>


### [209] [Back to the Future: Rethinking Endorsement in Order-Execute Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27659)
*Rongji Huang,Yifeng Ye,Gerui Wang,Mingchao Wan,Yuxing Duan,Jingjing Zhang,Guangtao Xue,Shengyun Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexTender框架，在传统order-execute架构中实现了灵活背书，避免了Hyperledger Fabric的EOV范式在高冲突负载下的高中止率问题，在以太坊USDT工作负载下吞吐量提升高达10.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代许可区块链需要灵活背书机制以支持合规和治理管理。Hyperledger Fabric采用的执行-排序-验证（EOV）范式在高冲突工作负载（如DeFi应用）中面临高中止率的限制。为避免重构现有系统并更好地适配DeFi应用，本文旨在将灵活背书集成到经典的排序-执行架构中。

Method: 提出FlexTender框架，在Tendermint BFT协议之上实现灵活背书。核心挑战是在保持抗审查性和去中心化的前提下，从有序列表中确定性移除问题交易。通过将背书优雅地嵌入共识过程，在正常情况下不产生额外消息开销。

Result: 使用以太坊USDT工作负载进行实证评估，FlexTender在相同平台上的吞吐量相比EOV模拟实现了高达10.6倍的加速。

Conclusion: 成功证明了可以在不改变经典order-execute架构的前提下实现灵活背书，既避免了EOV范式的高中止率问题，又保持了系统性能，为DeFi等高冲突应用场景提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Due to regulatory compliance and governance management, modern (permissioned) blockchains require flexible endorsement, which allows the endorsement policy for each contract or state object to be individually defined. To enable flexible endorsement, Hyperledger Fabric employs an execute-order-validate (EOV) paradigm, in which transactions first undergo speculative execution and endorsement, and are only then ordered and validated. Meanwhile, most blockchain systems, including the platform targeted in this work (i.e., ChainMaker), still follow a conflict-free order-execute framework. We argue that the EOV paradigm still faces several limitations, notably high abort rates in high-contention workloads such as those in Decentralized Finance (DeFi).
  To avoid refactoring our system and better suit DeFi applications, we try to integrate flexible endorsement into the classical order-execute architecture and accordingly propose a new framework. The key challenge is to deterministically remove problematic transactions from an ordered list, while preserving censorship resistance and decentralization for the remaining ones. We instantiate this framework on top of Tendermint, a seminal Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) protocol adopted in our system, and thereby propose FlexTender. By elegantly embedding endorsements into consensus, FlexTender incurs no additional messaging overhead in the normal case. Empirical evaluation using an Ethereum USDT workload demonstrates that FlexTender achieves up to $10.6\times$ speedup in throughput over an EOV simulation on the same platform.

</details>


### [210] [Monadic Presburger Predicates have Robust Population Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27767)
*Philipp Czerner,Javier Esparza,Vincent Fischer,Roland Guttenberg,Julian Pins,Simon Reilich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了单子Presburger算术可表达的所有谓词都存在鲁棒种群协议，并发现鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价至少是双指数级的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Lossin等人提出的关于对抗性崩溃故障下种群协议鲁棒性的开放问题，即是否每个Presburger谓词都能由鲁棒协议判定，本文旨在推进该问题的解决。

Method: 证明了所有可用单子Presburger算术表达的谓词都存在鲁棒协议；通过研究判定给定谓词的最小鲁棒协议与最小协议的状态数量比值，分析了鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价。

Result: 证明了单子Presburger算术可表达的所有谓词都具有鲁棒协议；发现鲁棒性的状态复杂度代价至少是谓词大小的双指数级；证明了Lossin等人针对阈值谓词x >= k的鲁棒协议具有最优状态复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文部分解决了Lossin等人提出的开放问题，表明至少单子Presburger算术范围内的谓词都存在鲁棒协议，同时量化了实现鲁棒性所需的显著状态复杂度开销。

Abstract: Population protocols are a model of distributed computation in which a collection of indistinguishable finite-state agents interact randomly in pairs to decide a predicate of their initial configuration. The agents decide by achieving a stable consensus on whether the predicate holds or not. It is known that population protocols can decide exactly the predicates expressible in Presburger arithmetic.
  Recently, Lossin et al. have introduced a notion of protocol robustness against adversarial crash failures. They show that all atomic Presburger predicates can be decided by robust protocols, and ask whether the same holds for every Presburger predicate. We make progress towards settling this question by proving that all predicates expressible in monadic Presburger arithmetic have robust protocols. In addition, we analyze the cost of robustness in terms of state complexity. We study the ratio between the number of states of the smallest robust protocol for a given predicate and the smallest protocol for it. We show that the cost of robustness is at least double exponential in the size of the predicate, and prove that the robust protocols by Lossin et al. for threshold predicates x >= k have optimal state complexity.

</details>


### [211] [ZipCCL: Efficient Lossless Data Compression of Communication Collectives for Accelerating LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27844)
*Wenxiang Lin,Xinglin Pan,Ruibo Fan,Shaohuai Shi,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZipCCL，一个针对大语言模型（LLM）分布式训练的无损压缩通信库。通过利用LLM训练数据近似高斯分布的特性，结合理论驱动的指数编码、GPU优化内核及自适应通信策略，ZipCCL在64 GPU集群上实现了最高1.35倍的通信时间减少和1.18倍的端到端训练加速，且不影响模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信已成为大语言模型分布式训练的关键瓶颈。尽管已有多种方法尝试减少通信开销，但无损压缩的潜力未被充分挖掘，原因在于传统的压缩与解压缩开销往往超过了减少通信流量带来的收益。作者观察到LLM训练中的通信数据（包括激活值、梯度和参数）常呈现近似高斯分布，这为高效数据压缩提供了关键特征。

Method: 提出了ZipCCL无损压缩通信库，包含三项核心技术：（1）理论驱动的指数编码，利用LLM张量的高斯分布特性加速压缩，避免了昂贵的在线统计计算；（2）GPU优化的压缩与解压缩内核，通过精心设计的内存访问模式和基于通信感知的数据布局实现流水线处理；（3）自适应通信策略，根据工作负载模式和系统特性动态切换集合通信操作。

Result: 在64 GPU集群上，使用混合专家模型和密集Transformer模型进行评估，ZipCCL将通信时间减少了最高1.35倍，并实现了最高1.18倍的端到端训练加速，同时对模型质量没有任何影响。

Conclusion: ZipCCL证明了通过利用LLM训练数据的统计特性并结合GPU优化技术，无损压缩可以有效降低分布式训练中的通信瓶颈，实现显著的训练加速，为LLM的高效分布式训练提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Communication has emerged as a critical bottleneck in the distributed training of large language models (LLMs). While numerous approaches have been proposed to reduce communication overhead, the potential of lossless compression has remained largely underexplored since compression and decompression typically consume larger overheads than the benefits of reduced communication traffic. We observe that the communication data, including activations, gradients and parameters, during training often follows a near-Gaussian distribution, which is a key feature for data compression. Thus, we introduce ZipCCL, a lossless compressed communication library of collectives for LLM training. ZipCCL is equipped with our novel techniques: (1) theoretically grounded exponent coding that exploits the Gaussian distribution of LLM tensors to accelerate compression without expensive online statistics, (2) GPU-optimized compression and decompression kernels that carefully design memory access patterns and pipeline using communication-aware data layout, and (3) adaptive communication strategies that dynamically switch collective operations based on workload patterns and system characteristics. Evaluated on a 64-GPU cluster using both mixture-of-experts and dense transformer models, ZipCCL reduces communication time by up to 1.35$\times$ and achieves end-to-end training speedups of up to 1.18$\times$ without any impact on model quality.

</details>


### [212] [AI Inference as Relocatable Electricity Demand: A Latency-Constrained Energy-Geography Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27855)
*Xubin Luo,Yang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个能源-地理框架，通过优化AI推理任务在地理分布式计算节点间的放置，实现延迟约束下的电力需求迁移，以降低成本和碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理正成为一种持续且地理分布广泛的电力需求来源。与许多传统电力负载不同，推理工作负载有时可以在远离面向用户服务位置的地方执行，前提是延迟、状态局部性、容量和监管约束仍然可接受。本文研究这种计算的数字迁移何时可以解释为延迟约束下的电力需求迁移。

Method: 开发了一个用于地理分布式AI推理的能源-地理框架。该框架建模了客户端、服务节点和计算节点的三层架构，并将推理放置问题形式化为一个受约束的优化问题，考虑因素包括电价、边际碳强度、电力使用效率、计算容量、网络延迟和迁移摩擦。核心概念是能源-延迟前沿：通过放宽推理延迟预算所能释放的边际成本和碳效益。引入了可重定位推理需求、延迟的能源回报、延迟的碳回报以及重置盈亏平衡条件等运营指标。

Result: 通过对代表性全球计算区域进行透明的风格化模拟，结果显示延迟放宽扩大了可行地理范围。同时，迁移摩擦、出口成本、状态局部性、法律约束和容量限制会显著减少已实现的效益。研究揭示了异构延迟容忍度如何将工作负载分为本地、区域能源导向执行层。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在延迟约束下重新定位AI推理计算以优化能源使用和碳排放的可行性，同时也强调了实际部署中需要克服的摩擦和约束因素。能源-延迟前沿为理解和量化延迟与能源效益之间的权衡提供了新的视角。

Abstract: AI inference is becoming a persistent and geographically distributed source of electricity demand. Unlike many traditional electrical loads, inference workloads can sometimes be executed away from the user-facing service location, provided that latency, state locality, capacity, and regulatory constraints remain acceptable. This paper studies when such digital relocation of computation can be interpreted as latency-constrained relocation of electricity demand.
  We develop an energy-geography framework for geo-distributed AI inference. The framework models a three-layer architecture of clients, service nodes, and compute nodes, and formulates inference placement as a constrained optimization problem over electricity prices, marginal carbon intensity, power usage effectiveness, compute capacity, network latency, and migration frictions. The key object is the energy-latency frontier: the marginal cost and carbon benefit unlocked by relaxing inference latency budgets.
  The paper makes four contributions. First, it distinguishes physical electricity transmission from digital relocation of electricity-consuming computation. Second, it formulates a geo-distributed inference placement model with feasibility masks and migration frictions. Third, it introduces operational metrics, including relocatable inference demand, energy return on latency, carbon return on latency, and a relocation break-even condition. Fourth, it provides a transparent stylized simulation over representative global compute regions to show how heterogeneous latency tolerance separates workloads into local, regional, and energy-oriented execution layers.
  The results show that latency relaxation expands feasible geography, while migration frictions, egress costs, state locality, legal constraints, and capacity limits can sharply reduce realized benefits.

</details>


### [213] [AnTi-MiCS: Analytical Framework for Bounding Time in Embedded Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27862)
*Behnaz Ranjbar,Akash Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对混合关键性系统中低关键性模式下的WCET确定难题，提出了AnTi-MiCS和MulTi-MiCS两种分析方法，在真实平台上实现了QoS提升30.27%、利用率浪费减少35.89%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合关键性（MC）系统中，高关键性任务的WCET作为保守上界虽然保证了安全性，但低关键性（LO）模式下如何确定合适的低WCET是一个关键挑战：过低的WCET虽能提高处理器利用率，但会导致频繁的模式切换从而降低服务质量；过高的WCET虽能减少模式切换，但会降低利用率。这种利用率与服务质量之间的权衡需要通过合理的低WCET设置来解决。

Method: 提出了两种分析方法：（1）AnTi-MiCS：通过设计时任务执行分析来确定单一合适的低WCET；（2）MulTi-MiCS：针对执行时间分布存在大变异（如双峰分布）的场景，利用连续输入之间的时间相关性，计算多个合适的低WCET值，以提高方法的适应性和可扩展性。

Result: 在真实嵌入式实时基准测试平台上进行的实验表明：与现有方法相比，AnTi-MiCS平均提升QoS 30.27%，减少利用率浪费35.89%；MulTi-MiCS相比AnTi-MiCS进一步将QoS提升6.41%，利用率浪费减少8.23%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分析方法有效解决了MC系统中LO模式下WCET确定问题，在保证系统安全性的同时实现了利用率与服务质量的良好平衡，为混合关键性系统的设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In Mixed-Criticality (MC) systems, although the high Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) serves as a conservative upper bound representing the task's maximum execution time under all conditions, obtaining a low WCET is essential for representing realistic executions and improving utilization and Quality-of-Service (QoS). Nevertheless, determining appropriate low WCET(s) for lower-criticality (LO) modes poses a significant challenge. Opting for a very low value of this WCET enhances processor utilization by scheduling more tasks in LO mode. Conversely, employing a larger WCET ensures fewer mode switches, thereby enhancing QoS, albeit at the cost of processor utilization. This paper proposes an analytical approach, AnTi-MiCS, to determine the appropriate low WCET through design-time analysis of task executions. In some cases, a single low WCET may not be adequate to capture large variations in the execution time distribution, for example, in scenarios like bimodal distributions. Therefore, we further propose a scalable approach, MulTi-MiCS, to compute multiple appropriate low WCETs. This approach exploits the temporal correlation between subsequent inputs presented to the application. Experimental results, conducted on a real platform with embedded real-time benchmarks, demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed scheme, in which QoS is improved by 30.27% on average while reducing utilization waste by 35.89%, compared to existing approaches. Besides, MulTi-MiCS improves QoS by 6.41% compared to AnTi-MiCS while reducing utilization waste by 8.23%.

</details>


### [214] [The Origins of MEV: Systematic Attribution of Arbitrage Opportunity Creation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27979)
*Andrei Seoev,Dmitry Belousov,Anastasiia Smirnova,Ksenia Kurinova,Aleksei Smirnov,Denis Fedyanin,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了MEV机会归因问题，提出了四种归因方法，并通过大规模实验发现大多数原子套利机会可追溯至单一源头交易，且MEV创造分布高度集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注MEV的提取与缓解，但缺乏系统性方法将MEV机会归因至其链上源头。为了填补这一空白，需要建立能够识别哪些交易创造了套利机会并量化其贡献的框架。

Method: 论文首先形式化了MEV机会归因问题，随后设计并评估了四种针对EVM兼容网络原子套利的归因方法：基于机器人数据的方法、基于仿真的方法、基于系数的方法以及基于Shapley值的方法。

Result: 在Polygon网络超过一百万个区块的大规模回溯分析中，研究表明大多数原子套利机会可追溯到单一源头交易，验证了竞争性MEV市场的核心假设。此外，研究量化了MEV创造的高度集中分布，发现少数协议产生了大部分机会，并分析了各归因方法在准确性、成本和可扩展性方面的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果为协议设计者减少MEV泄露、验证者优化交易排序以及分析师通过机会创造衡量生态系统健康提供了重要见解，有助于更好地理解和治理区块链网络中的MEV现象。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) represents billions of dollars in extracted value that fundamentally shapes blockchain network dynamics and participant incentives. While research has focused on MEV extraction and mitigation, we lack systematic methods to attribute MEV opportunities to their on-chain origins. This paper formalizes the MEV opportunity attribution problem and introduces a systems framework for identifying which transactions create arbitrage opportunities and quantifying their contributions. We design and evaluate four attribution methods for atomic arbitrage on EVM-compatible networks: bot-data-driven, simulation-based, coefficient-based, and Shapley-based approaches. Through large-scale retrospective analysis spanning over one million blocks on Polygon, we demonstrate that the majority of atomic arbitrage opportunities can be traced to single source transactions, validating our central hypothesis about competitive MEV markets. We quantify a highly concentrated distribution of MEV creation, where a small subset of protocols generates most opportunities, and provide comparative analysis of method trade-offs in accuracy, cost, and scalability. Our findings offer insights for protocol designers reducing MEV leakage, validators optimizing transaction ordering, and analysts measuring ecosystem health through opportunity creation.

</details>


### [215] [Exploring Sparse Matrix Multiplication Kernels on the Cerebras CS-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27985)
*Milan Shah,Sheng Di,Michela Becchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Cerebras CS-3 AI加速器上优化稀疏线性代数核SpMM和SDDMM的性能，实现了相比CPU最高100倍和20倍的加速，但发现在极高稀疏度（>99%）下会出现性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cerebras CS-3作为一种新型AI加速器，在大模型训练和科学计算等密集计算任务上已展现出优异性能，但其在稀疏计算任务（如图神经网络、线性求解器和推荐系统）方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 针对SpMM和SDDMM这两个关键稀疏线性代数核，提出了底层CS-3内核设计方案，并从I/O性能、内存占用和大规模矩阵可扩展性三个维度进行了优化。

Result: 对于90%稀疏度的矩阵，CS-3在SpMM上比CPU快100倍，在SDDMM上比CPU快20倍，且性能随矩阵维度增加而提升；但当稀疏度超过99%时，CS-3出现性能下降，SpMM速度甚至低于CPU。

Conclusion: CS-3加速器在中高稀疏度场景下具有显著的性能优势，能够有效加速稀疏计算工作负载，但在处理极高稀疏度矩阵时存在性能瓶颈，需要针对极端稀疏场景进行进一步优化。

Abstract: In recent years, novel AI accelerators have emerged as promising alternatives to GPU for AI model training and inference tasks. One such accelerator, the Cerebras CS-3, achieves strong performance on large model training as well as scientific applications like molecular dynamics simulations. While dense compute workloads have been thoroughly explored for the CS-3, its potential for sparse workloads has not been fully examined. Applications requiring sparse linear algebra kernels, such as GNNs, linear solvers, and recommendation systems, could achieve good performance on a dataflow accelerator like the CS-3.
  In this work, we explore two key sparse linear algebra kernels, sparse-dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) and sampled dense-dense matrix multiplication (SDDMM), on the Cerebras CS-3. We propose low-level CS-3 kernel designs for these operations and optimize our designs to improve I/O performance, memory footprint, and scalability to large matrices. Our evaluation examines memory footprint and SpMM/SDDMM speedup relative to CPU. The evaluation suggests that the CS-3 can outperform CPU by 100$\times$ for SpMM with 90\% sparse matrices with performance improving as sparse matrix dimensionality increases. SDDMM on CS-3 can outperform CPU 20$\times$ for 90\% sparse matrices. We additionally find that as sparsity increases to beyond 99\%, the CS-3 suffers from performance degradation that makes it slower than CPU for SpMM.

</details>


### [216] [From Impermanent Loss to Sustainable Gain: Quantifying Profitability Zones for Liquidity Providers on DEX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28014)
*Ignat Melnikov,Roman Vlasov,Vladimir Gorgadze,Andrey Seoev,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于AMM的DEX协议，通过建立数学模型分析套利者与流动性池的交互，推导了LP与套利者的联合收益界限，提出了预估非永久性损失发生时间的方法，并确定了使流动性提供者保持在盈利区所需的最低手续费阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）和自动做市商（AMM）协议虽然提供了创新的金融工具，但也引入了新的市场低效性，易被套利者系统性利用，导致流动性提供者（LP）面临风险。现有风险评估方法不足，亟需量化LP与套利者的共生关系及风险。

Method: 基于经验池配置建立数学模型，分析套利者与AMM流动性池的交互机制；推导LP与套利者联合收益的理论界限；开发估计非永久性损失（IL）预期发生时间的方法；计算维持非永久性收益（IG）状态所需的最低手续费下限。

Result: 提出了LP与套利者联合收益的界限；建立了预估非永久性损失发生时间的方法；获得了确保LP在特定概率下保持盈利所需的最低手续费下界；该框架扩展了现有LP风险评估方法。

Conclusion: 该研究通过量化LP与套利者的共生盈利区域，为手续费选择提供了原则性依据，有助于协调LP与套利者激励机制，提升市场稳定性，并为DeFi风险管理提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) is a rapidly evolving segment of blockchain technology that enables a transformative approach to financial services through Web3 applications. By leveraging smart contracts, DeFi allows developers to build flexible and innovative financial instruments. Among the most prominent DeFi primitives by liquidity are decentralized exchange~(DEX) swap protocols~(such as Uniswap, Curve, and Balancer) that facilitate fast token-to-token exchanges. However, new exchange mechanisms also introduce new market inefficiencies that can be systematically exploited by arbitrageurs. This paper focuses on swap protocols based on the Automated Market Maker~(AMM), where the product of reserves is preserved as an invariant. We analyze the interaction between arbitrageurs and AMM liquidity pools and develop a mathematical model grounded in empirical pool configurations. Using this model, we derive bounds on the joint revenue of liquidity providers~(LPs) and arbitrageurs, propose a method to estimate the expected number of blocks until the occurrence of Impermanent Loss~(IL), and obtain a lower bound on the pool fee required to achieve a fixed target probability of staying in the Impermanent Gain (IG) zone within a block. The proposed framework extends existing LP risk-assessment methodologies by quantifying symbiotic profitability zones, providing a principled basis for fee selection that aligns LP-arbitrageur incentives and enhances market stability.

</details>


### [217] [Characterizing Path-Independent Fees: A Route to Zero Impermanent Loss in CPMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28017)
*Andrey Voronin,Roman Vlasov,Vladimir Gorgadze,Andrey Seoev,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究自动做市商（AMM）中费用再投资导致的路径依赖问题，确定了保证路径独立的费用函数类别，提出了能够消除无常损失的参数化费用结构，并证明了不存在对所有初始状态通用的零无常损失费用函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Uniswap V2等恒定乘积做市商中，交易费用通常按固定比例收取并自动再投资至流动性池。这种机制导致交易的最终状态依赖于交易被拆分的方式，即存在路径依赖性。路径依赖性不仅增加了流动性提供者风险评估的复杂性，还影响了DeFi协议之间的可组合性保证。因此，识别能够消除路径依赖性的费用结构具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Method: 作者首先刻画了确保路径独立性的费用函数类：组合费用因子必须仅依赖于当前池不变量k=xy。在该函数类内，推导出控制池动态的常微分方程组，并获得封闭形式的积分交换公式。基于此框架，构建了一个参数化费用函数族，使其在给定初始池状态下实现零无常损失。此外，通过理论分析证明了不存在能够对所有初始状态同时消除无常损失的通用费用函数。最后，通过受控仿真验证了理论结果。

Result: 研究结果表明：路径独立性要求费用函数仅依赖于池不变量k；在该约束下可推导池动态的解析解；提出的参数化费用函数族能在特定初始状态下实现零无常损失；但理论上证明了不存在对任意初始状态通用的零无常损失费用函数；同时分析了费用结构对套利窗口和滑点的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为AMM协议设计提供了理论框架，指导如何在保持路径独立性和可组合性的前提下优化费用结构。研究结果揭示了零无常损失费用函数的存在条件与局限，为流动性提供者和交易者激励对齐提供了原理性指导。

Abstract: Constant Product Market Makers use fees that are typically fixed proportions of trade size. When these fees are automatically reinvested into the pool, as in Uniswap~V2 and some designs of Uniswap V4, the final state after a trade can depend on how the trade is split into smaller transactions. This path dependence complicates the risk assessment for liquidity providers and affects composability guarantees. We characterize the functional class of fee structures that ensure path independence: the combined fee factor must depend only on the current pool invariant k=xy. For this class, we derive a system of ordinary differential equations governing pool dynamics and obtain a closed-form integral exchange formula. Within this class, we construct a parametric family of fee functions that achieve zero Impermanent Loss for a given initial pool state, and prove that no universal fee function can eliminate Impermanent Loss for all initial states simultaneously. We analyze implications for arbitrage windows and slippage, and validate our theory through controlled simulations. Our framework provides protocol designers with a principled approach to fee optimization that aligns liquidity provider and trader incentives while preserving composability.

</details>


### [218] [Akita: A High Usability Simulation Framework for Computer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28073)
*Sabila Al Jannat,Ying Li,Mengyang He,Xuzhong Wang,Huizhi Zhao,Jingxiang Sun,Daoxuan Xu,Enze Xu,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Akita仿真引擎，通过将基础设施与硬件模型解耦，解决了计算机架构仿真器在使用和开发上的障碍，使开发者只需编写简单的单线程代码即可自动获得高性能和并行化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机架构仿真器虽然功能强大，但在使用和开发上存在重大障碍，主要表现为组件接口不规范、部署要求严格、组件级性能优化负担重、监控和可视化能力有限。其根本原因在于系统性地忽视了用户和开发者体验，过于偏重技术功能。

Method: 提出了Akita专用仿真引擎，采用清晰的关注点分离设计，将基础设施与架构模型解耦。核心技术包括：Smart Ticking和Availability Backpropagation机制，使开发者能编写简单的基于周期的代码同时获得事件驱动的性能；透明的并行仿真，开发者编写单线程代码而Akita自动处理多核执行；统一而强大的仿真追踪支持，实现实时监控和仿真后可视化。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了Akita的灵活性，包括基于追踪的DNN仿真和RISC-V CPU仿真开发。证明了优先考虑开发者体验可以加速架构研究。Smart Ticking和Availability Backpropagation成功实现了简单代码与高性能的统一，并行仿真机制透明有效。

Conclusion: 只有通过专用的仿真引擎将技术关注点与用户体验关注点分离，才能克服现有仿真器的基本障碍，实现更高效的架构研究。Akita的成功证明了这种设计哲学的有效性，为未来的仿真器设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Computer architecture simulation is essential for evaluating new designs without the need for costly tapeout. The community has developed dozens of valuable simulators that have enabled significant architectural advances. However, using and developing simulators remains a major barrier due to ad-hoc component interfaces, strict deployment requirements, the burden of managing performance optimizations like parallelization at the component level, and limited monitoring and visualization capabilities. The root cause of these limitations is the systematic neglect of user and developer experience in favor of technical functionality. We believe that only by separating technical concerns from user and developer experience concerns -- through a dedicated simulation engine decoupled from hardware models -- can the community overcome these fundamental obstacles and enable more productive architectural research.
  Akita embodies this philosophy as a dedicated simulation engine that cleanly separates infrastructure from architectural models. Smart Ticking and Availability Backpropagation let developers write simple cycle-based code while achieving event-driven performance. Parallel simulation happens transparently -- developers write single-threaded code while Akita handles multi-core execution. Akita's simple, uniform, yet powerful simulation tracing support enables real-time monitoring and post-simulation visualization. We demonstrate the flexibility of Akita through case studies, including the development of a trace-based DNN simulation and a RISC-V CPU simulation, showing how prioritizing developer experience accelerates architectural research.

</details>


### [219] [Agentic Compilation: Mitigating the LLM Rerun Crisis for Minimized-Inference-Cost Web Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09718)
*Jagadeesh Chundru*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出“编译-执行”架构，将LLM推理与浏览器执行解耦，解决了传统连续推理代理在重复任务中的“重演危机”，将推理成本从O(M×N)降至均摊O(1)，实现了经济可行的大规模Web自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续推理循环的LLM Web代理在执行重复性任务时面临“重演危机”问题：随着执行频率增加，Token消耗和API延迟呈线性增长，导致成本高昂且可扩展性差。一个5步工作流在500次迭代中可能产生约150美元的推理成本，即使使用激进缓存仍需约15美元。

Method: 提出“编译-执行”架构：(1) 单次LLM调用处理来自DOM清洗模块(DSM)的高效语义表示，输出确定性JSON工作流蓝图；(2) 轻量级运行时驱动浏览器执行，无需后续模型查询。该架构实现了推理与执行的解耦，通过模块化JSON中间表示支持最小化的人工介入(HITL)修补。

Result: 在数据提取、表单填充和指纹识别任务中，零样本编译成功率达80-94%；通过HITL修补可将执行可靠性提升至接近100%；单次编译成本在五个前沿模型中介于0.002-0.092美元，将每工作流推理成本降至0.10美元以下。

Conclusion: 确定性编译范式成功将推理扩展从O(M×N)降至均摊O(1)，使原本在连续架构下不可行的规模化Web自动化成为经济可行的现实。

Abstract: LLM-driven web agents operating through continuous inference loops -- repeatedly querying a model to evaluate browser state and select actions -- exhibit a fundamental scalability constraint for repetitive tasks. We characterize this as the Rerun Crisis: the linear growth of token expenditure and API latency relative to execution frequency. For a 5-step workflow over 500 iterations, a continuous agent incurs approximately 150.00 USD in inference costs; even with aggressive caching, this remains near 15.00 USD. We propose a Compile-and-Execute architecture that decouples LLM reasoning from browser execution, reducing per-workflow inference cost to under 0.10 USD. A one-shot LLM invocation processes a token-efficient semantic representation from a DOM Sanitization Module (DSM) and emits a deterministic JSON workflow blueprint. A lightweight runtime then drives the browser without further model queries. We formalize this cost reduction from O(M x N) to amortized O(1) inference scaling, where M is the number of reruns and N is the sequential actions. Empirical evaluation across data extraction, form filling, and fingerprinting tasks yields zero-shot compilation success rates of 80-94%. Crucially, the modularity of the JSON intermediate representation allows minimal Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) patching to elevate execution reliability to near-100%. At per-compilation costs between 0.002 USD and 0.092 USD across five frontier models, these results establish deterministic compilation as a paradigm enabling economically viable automation at scales previously infeasible under continuous architectures.

</details>
